# Agent Coordination — READ BEFORE EDITING

This repo is edited by multiple AI agents (Claude in chat, Kilo in VS Code,
Cline in VS Code, and a scheduled Claude Cowork task) plus Lujane directly.
To avoid silent conflicts, every agent follows this protocol.

## Before you touch anything

1. **Pull `main` fresh.** Never work from a copy fetched earlier in your
   own session — another agent may have committed since then.
2. **Read the "Current Status" section below.** If another agent shows
   `IN PROGRESS` on a file you need to touch, stop and flag it to Lujane
   instead of editing anyway.
3. **Update "Current Status" to declare yourself IN PROGRESS** before you
   start editing, listing exactly which files.

## While working

- Keep changes small and scoped to the actual task. Don't refactor,
  rename, or restructure things that weren't asked for.
- Don't touch another agent's in-progress files.
- Test before committing (JSON validity, JS syntax, YAML validity —
  whichever applies).

## After committing

- Update "Current Status" back to `IDLE`.
- Add one entry to the top of the "Recent Activity Log": date, agent,
  files, one-line summary. Keep the last ~15 entries; trim older ones.
- Report to Lujane: files changed, commit SHA, what you tested, anything
  left open.

## Known regular actors

- **Claude (chat)** — primary builder, holds a GitHub PAT scoped to this
  repo, pushes via the GitHub API directly.
- **Kilo / Cline (VS Code)** — edit locally via Lujane's own git
  credentials.
- **Claude Cowork (scheduled task)** — runs on a timer, only ever touches
  `data/sports.json`, `data/gatherings.json`, `data/resources.json`
  (formerly also `data/organizations.json`, which was removed 2026-08-25).
  Never touches `index.html`, `admin/`, or `calendar.ics`. If you see
  unfamiliar entries in those files, that's almost certainly this task,
  not a bug.

---

## ⚠️ Live site deploys manually — this is the #1 thing to know

`4dasistas.ca` is served by a Cloudflare **Worker** named `4dasistas` (not
Cloudflare Pages). As of 2026-08-21, pushing to `main` does **not**
redeploy automatically — someone has to run `wrangler deploy` by hand, or
connect Git integration. This is what caused the "everything disappeared"
scare on 2026-08-21 — nothing was deleted, the live site was just frozen
on an old build.

**One-time fix (do this once, then every push auto-deploys):**
1. Cloudflare dashboard → Workers & Pages → `4dasistas` Worker → Settings → Build.
2. Connect the `lujaneyaffa/4dasistas` GitHub repo, branch `main`.
3. Build command: `python3 scripts/build_content.py && python3 scripts/generate_calendar.py`
   **(both scripts, in that order — build_content.py merges the per-entry
   `data/calendar/*.json` / `data/resources/*.json` files the CMS writes
   into the aggregate `data/*.json` files this site actually reads. Skip
   it and every new CMS entry silently never shows up on the live site —
   this is exactly what happened 2026-08-25, see the log entry below.)**
4. Deploy command: `npx wrangler deploy` (`wrangler.toml` at repo root defines the asset deploy).
5. Save.

**Until that's connected**, deploy manually after any merge to `main`:
```
python3 scripts/build_content.py
python3 scripts/generate_calendar.py
npx wrangler deploy
```

**2026-10-02 (Joining a club now needs admin approval; admin emailed on every request) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Lujane: "random ppl can just select what club they're in. make it so they have to request and I accept from my side. don't change existing members, only from now on. also I receive notifications on email every time someone requests." **Problem:** the "which club are you in" picker was only a localStorage view preference (anyone could tick any club and see its feed), AND self-signup (`POST /api/signup` with a `clubId`) dropped people straight into their #1 club's member list. **Fix:** real membership (`clubmembers:{clubId}`, which is what existing members already have and what admin-created profiles use) is now the single source of truth; the picker and its localStorage are gone. New: `POST /api/clubs/:id/join-request` (signed-in member; one KV key per club+person, `joinreq:{clubId}:{userId}`, so simultaneous requests can't clobber each other; max 4 pending per person), `GET /api/me` (fresh clubs + pending requests; the cached session goes stale after approval), and admin-only `GET /api/admin/join-requests` + `POST /api/admin/join-requests/:clubId/:userId/(accept|decline)` (added to the `requiresAuth` list; verified 401 without login). **Signup change:** a signup's #1 club pick becomes a pending request instead of instant membership (the account, standby list and the Formspree form to organizers are unchanged); the form copy now says an admin approves the #1 pick. Existing members are untouched (tested: an admin-created member kept their club and only sees the others as requestable). **UI:** signed-in Clubs page = Change PIN, a "waiting for approval" banner if any, CLUBS YOU'RE IN (real memberships) + availability + vote bubbles, "REQUEST ANOTHER CLUB" rows (button, then "Requested ✓ waiting for approval"), then the WhatsApp disclaimer (reworded to "request"); someone in no club sees "You're not in a club yet" + the request list. Admin panel gets a "Join requests (N)" card at the top with Accept/Decline. **Email:** `notifyAdminOfJoinRequest` sends through the same Resend setup as the daily list, to the new Worker secret `ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL` (kept out of the repo on purpose); if it or `RESEND_API_KEY` is unset the request still saves and shows in the admin panel, it just can't email. **Not changed (flagging):** a club's own idea board page is still open to anyone — including name-only guests — to view/vote/add, by original design; this change only controls who counts as *in* a club (the "Clubs you're in" dashboard + feed). Also: declining deletes the request, so a declined person can immediately ask again (the 4-pending cap doesn't limit re-asking after a decline). **Tested (local worker + browser):** existing member unaffected; new signup -> clubs [] + pending [club]; /api/me; request second club, duplicate request idempotent, unknown club 404, no token 401; admin list/accept/decline, double-accept 404, accepted person's page picks up the club after the one-time /api/me refresh while the other request stays pending; admin card renders and its buttons hit the right URLs. Email delivery could not be tested locally. **UPDATE after deploy — email is NOT working yet:** I set the `ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL` Worker secret (to the Cloudflare account email, lujanealdimasi@gmail.com) but `wrangler secret list` shows **`RESEND_API_KEY` has never been set on the production Worker** (only ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL, ADMIN_PASSWORD, CLUB_SIGNUP_PIN, GITHUB_TOKEN exist), so `notifyAdminOfJoinRequest` silently does nothing — and the existing daily-list email (`scheduled()`) has been silently inert for the same reason. Needs Lujane to create/connect a Resend account (and verify 4dasistas.ca as a sender) and run `wrangler secret put RESEND_API_KEY` (or paste it in the Cloudflare dashboard) — I deliberately did not create the account or handle the API key. Until then she sees requests only by logging in as admin (Join requests card). Verified live: signup -> pending, /api/me, join-request stored in KV, admin endpoints 401 without login; my throwaway test requests were deleted from KV afterward. (First secret upload had a stray trailing "." from my parsing — caught and re-uploaded correctly.)

**2026-10-03 (Bubbles toned down: calm drift, smooth hover nudge) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `service-worker.js`** — Lujane: the drift was intense and glitchy; hover should move them slightly. **2026-10-03 (Web push notifications + required phone confirmation + 'last reminded' disclaimer + livelier bubbles) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`, `service-worker.js`** — Lujane asked for FREE phone notifications as a new requirement. (A) Web Push: the Worker sends an EMPTY push (no payload encryption needed) authenticated with VAPID (ES256 JWT signed by Worker secret `VAPID_PRIVATE_JWK`, which I generated and set; public key is hard-coded in the Worker + `index.html`); the service worker's `push` handler fetches the text from `GET /api/push/message?e=<sha256(endpoint)>` (message stored in KV `pushmsg:*`, 24h) and shows it; `notificationclick` opens the link. Members register devices via `POST /api/push/subscribe` (KV `push:{userId}`, max 5 devices; 404/410 from the push service drops a dead device). `/api/me` returns `push:boolean`. Admin: `POST /api/admin/push/send {userIds,title,body,url}` (INSIDE the auth gate — I first placed it before the gate and caught it with an unauth test, then moved it), `/api/admin/users` now has `push` per person, accept/decline of a join request also pushes the person. Admin UI: '📲 Push notifications' card (stats, 'Who hasn't?' with WhatsApp nudge links, audience = everyone-with-push / hasn't-added-availability / each club, title+message, send) and a '📲 Send push reminder' button in the calendar's missing-availability box. (B) Required for every member (not admin): `pushGateMode()` — on a phone not opened from the Home Screen -> blocking 'install' screen (iPhone Share>Add to Home Screen / Android Install app; on iOS push ONLY works from the installed app, and the installed app has separate storage so they log in again there); otherwise a blocking 'Turn on notifications' screen (user gesture -> permission -> subscribe). Shown after login/sign-up and on app open for existing sessions (old members hit it next time they open the app); devices with no push support aren't blocked; each gate has 'Message an admin' + 'Log out'. (C) Required once: confirm/enter phone number (`renderPhoneGate`, `POST /api/me/phone`, stores `phone` + `phoneConfirmedAt`; `/api/me` returns phone/phoneConfirmed); shown BEFORE the push gate; prefilled if we already have one. Also fixed: `cmGeneralLogin` returned early with an error for members who had no club (session set but UI not redrawn) — everyone now lands on the signed-in page. (D) 'Last reminded' disclaimer: server records `meta:lastRemind:app` / `meta:lastRemind:push` (when/how many) and `GET /api/admin/reminders/last`; shown above the Remind/Push buttons and repeated in each confirm dialog. (E) Bubbles: far bigger drift (±40-90px multi-point paths with slight rotation), and hovering ANYWHERE on the cloud switches EVERY bubble to a faster, much wider 'wild' path (`ideaFloatWild`). (F) Profile cards: if last-signed-in and last-active are within 10 min they show as ONE line ('Last signed in & active') — Lujane reported it 'shows twice'; I could not reproduce a duplicated card (22 profiles, no duplicate names, one block per card in a render test), so that was my best reading. TESTED: VAPID signature verifies against the published public key; subscribe/send/message/unauth/401 paths, phone + reminder-meta routes on the Worker locally; gates, hover motion and admin card in a mocked browser. **NOT TESTED: an actual push arriving on a real iPhone/Android** — needs Lujane to install the app and allow notifications, then use the admin card to send herself one. SW v27.

**2026-10-03 (Correction: the duplicate profiles were Lujane's own quick-login attempts) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — I first blamed the girls re-submitting; Lujane said nobody but her created them. Fix: when `state.cmAdminLoggedIn === true`, quick-login shows '✓ Created <username>…' and does NOT sign the admin in as her. In production the same girl had signed up repeatedly: Ayana S ×3 (ayana.s, ayana.s2, ayana.s3 — only s3 is in clubs) and Sabab M ×3 (sabab.m, sabab.m2 has availability, sabab.m3 in Quest) — likely forgot she already had a login/PIN and signed up again (the quick-login link made it easy). (2) ROOT CAUSE of the lingering row: KV `list()` is eventually consistent, so re-reading the pending list right after the delete still returned it. Fix: optimistic removal + a client-side `admNotif.dismissed` set that filters every later read (and `cmAdminLoadJoinRequests`). (3)(4) New admin notification centre: a fixed 🔔 bell (bottom-right, only when admin is logged in) with a red count badge = items newer than `localStorage admNotifSeenAt` (opening the panel marks seen); polls join-requests + event/resource suggestions every ~40s while the tab is visible; a new item triggers a top toast ('X just requested to join Y'); the panel lists everything with inline Accept/Decline (join) or Review (event/resource). (5) Calendar: yellow box '⏳ Haven't added their availability yet — <club> (N of M)' with profile links, recomputed for the selected club chip; chips carry a red count badge; ✅ when everyone has. Tested in browser with mocked network: dot->panel->accept (row gone, stale poll doesn't resurrect it, WhatsApp link correct), new-request toast+dot, member notice popup + ack, missing-list per club; and locally on the Worker (phone/at in list, notices after accept, ack clears, ack 401 w/o login). **2026-10-03 (Bug: same person listed 4x in the admin availability calendar) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane saw 'Lujane' four times in the admin calendar's name list. Cause: `readIdeaAvailPeople()` lists every KV key with prefix `ideaavail:`, which also returns the LEGACY per-club records (`ideaavail:club-activegaming:<id>`, `…club-artscrafts…`, `…club-retreats…`) written before availability became one global record on Oct 1. Nothing writes them now, but they were still read — so a person in several clubs appeared once per old record, and they also inflated the 'Everyone's availability' counts / respondent number. Fix: only keys whose id starts with `m:`, `g:` or is `admin` are read. The orphaned legacy KV keys are left in place (harmless, now ignored).

Built an importer instead: admin panel 'Import phone numbers' card — she exports the form's submissions as CSV from Formspree (Submissions -> Export) and picks the file; the page reads the Name + WhatsApp/phone columns (`parseCsv`) and posts `[{name, phone}]` to new `POST /api/admin/import-phones`, which matches on the normalised full name (fallback first-name + last-initial), skips ambiguous names, NEVER overwrites an existing phone, and reports updated / already-had / unmatched. **2026-10-03 (BUG: one person's availability leaked to the next login on the same browser) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `service-worker.js` + one production KV repair** — Lujane logged in, logged out, logged in as Sannah, and Sannah's schedule was identical to hers. Root cause: the availability state (`state.ideaAvail`, `ideaAvailNo`, the 'Everyone's availability' data, pending requests, etc.) lived in page memory and was NEVER reset on logout/login — `cmMemberSignOut` only called `availReset()` (the old per-club grid), and `ideaAvailLoad()` only fetches when `state.ideaAvail === null`, so Sannah's page showed Lujane's grid, and when Sannah edited/pressed Done it PUT Lujane's grid into HER record. Fix: `resetUserScopedState()` clears every per-person field and is called on logout AND whenever `setCmSession` is given a different user id than the previous session; logout first flushes the old person's pending save while still under their login; and `ideaAvailSave()` now refuses to write unless the grid was loaded for the currently-signed-in identity (`ideaAvailOwner` vs `ideaOwnerKey()`). Verified with a mocked two-user run: Lujane's unsaved edit saved to HER record at logout, Sannah started empty, no PUT for Sannah. **Data repair:** confirmed Sannah A's `ideaavail:m:6b2b017d…` doc was byte-identical (days AND no) to Lujane's, backed it up (scratchpad `sannah-availability-backup.json`) and DELETED it so she starts fresh (as 'not submitted') instead of being counted as available with Lujane's schedule. Other records were checked for duplicates: only that one (plus legacy per-club Lujane/Nurhan records from before the global-availability change, which nothing reads). SW cache v16.

**2026-10-03 (Email review links: full details + Accept / Edit / Deny; optional comments) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`, `service-worker.js`** — Lujane: notification emails for event/resource submissions should show the actual response and let her accept/deny/edit straight from the email via a direct link; plus an optional comments field on both forms. (1) Both forms got an optional `comments` textarea (stored on the suggestion, max 600 chars, shown in the email and admin cards). (2) `notifyAdminOfSuggestion` emails a table of every field + the comments + three buttons (✅ Accept / ✏️ Edit / ❌ Deny). (3) Buttons open `https://4dasistas.ca/review/{event|resource}/{id}?sig=…&a=…`: a no-login review page (server-rendered, `noindex`) with editable fields (event: name/date/city/virtual/link/Calendar tab; resource: name/type/link/city/virtual) and Accept & publish / Deny buttons. The link is signed: `sig = HMAC-SHA256(key 'review-link:'+ADMIN_PASSWORD, kind:id)` (`reviewSig`), compared constant-time; nothing is ever done on a GET (so email link-scanners can't accept/deny); actions are `POST /api/review/{kind}/{id}` {sig, action, fields}. Accept commits `data/calendar/<slug>.json` (event: title, section, eventDate, long `date`, location, virtual, link, desc=comments) or `data/resources/<slug>.json` (category=type, instagram|website=link, ownedBy 'Community submission') via the same `githubPutFile` as the admin editor — the normal build/deploy then publishes it (~1 min); Deny just deletes the KV submission. Admin cards also show comments and a 'Review' button (list endpoints now return `reviewUrl`). ANYONE holding the emailed link can accept/deny that one submission (it only goes to ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL); changing ADMIN_PASSWORD invalidates old links. Tested locally: page renders + HTML-escapes comments, bad sig 403, deny works then 'Already handled', accept validated up to the GITHUB_TOKEN step (no token locally). **The accept->GitHub commit path itself was NOT exercised until the first real use** — check the first accepted item landed. SW cache v14.

**2026-10-03 (Availability: green / red / yellow-unset + empty-submit reminder) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`, `service-worker.js`** — Lujane: every slot should be green or red (nothing white); people may submit empty slots but get a reminder that unfilled = available but not preferred. Model: per slot GREEN (`days`, preferred), RED (new `no` map, can't), or unset = shown pale YELLOW (`#fff3cf`, never white) and counted as AVAILABLE-but-not-preferred. NOTE: I used yellow for 'not filled' instead of forcing red/green, because her own rule says unfilled means available — red would contradict that; if she wants it red/green only, say so. Tap cycles unset -> green -> red -> unset (swipe paints; all-day button: all green -> all red -> not filled). Done editing with unset slots shows a one-time warning ('You didn't fill N slots across M days…') and the button becomes 'Done anyway'; submitting an all-empty grid still registers a response (`submitted`). Worker: docs are `{name, days, no}` (legacy 'busy' day => all slots red, `parseIdeaAvailDoc`); GET returns `{days,no,submitted}`; PUT sanitizes via `sanitizeIdeaAvail` (a slot can't be both; green wins; weekday mornings dropped). `GET /api/idea-availability/all` now returns `counts` = AVAILABLE per slot (green + unset-within-15-day-window, red excluded), plus `preferred`, `unavailable`, `windowDates`; respondents = everyone who submitted (even empty). Everyone's availability: heatmap = available count; best/worst timing rank by available then preferred / can't; tapping a square lists 🟢 preferred and 🟡 also-available names (red people hidden). Admin calendar person view: green/red/yellow bars. Tested: worker (3 users: counts/pref/no/legacy busy) and browser UI (no white cells, cycle, reminder, Done anyway PUTs). Not tested on a real phone. SW cache v11.

**2026-10-02 (Logout bug, PIN hiding/confirm, 'Log in as', phone numbers, header tagline) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — (1) BUG Lujane reported: after logging out she could still open Clubs and see 'logged in as Lujane'. Cause: `refreshMe()` (the /api/me refresh I added for join requests) captured the session before its fetch and, when the reply came back AFTER logout, did `setCmSession(...)` — silently re-creating the logged-out session. Fix: refreshMe re-reads the current session after the await and aborts if it's gone/different; `cmMemberSignOut` now also clears pending/me state and can't be blocked by a failing availability save; photo-update handlers no longer recreate a session when logged out. (2) PIN inputs are now `type=password` everywhere (login, sign-up, quick-login, choose-PIN, admin PIN fields); the sign-up and quick-login forms require typing the PIN twice (mismatch blocks submit). (3) 'Log in as <name>' button on the logged-out Clubs page (name+username remembered in localStorage `lastLogin` — never the PIN or token — so it only prefills the username and focuses the PIN; 'Not you?' forgets it). NOTE this deliberately does NOT log in without a PIN: keeping a token after logout would be the very bug above. (4) Phone numbers: sign-up now stores the WhatsApp number as `user.phone` (validated 7–15 digits, via `sanitizePhone`); it's returned ONLY by the admin `GET /api/admin/club-members` (+ editable via admin PUT), never by login/me/member endpoints (tested: login response has no phone). Admin cards show 📞 tap-to-call or 'No phone on file'. Existing members have no phone (it was only ever in Formspree) — admin can type it in Edit. (5) Removed the header tagline '#1st and only community…' (`.header-disclaimer` element).

Two parts: (1) BUG — when I moved 'Everyone's availability' onto the signed-in Clubs homepage I didn't also call `wireEveryoneAvailGrid()` there (it was only wired on the club page), so on the homepage the data never loaded and tapping squares did nothing; now wired in the signed-in branch. (2) `GET /api/idea-availability/all` now filters `people` AND the counts for a member viewer to people who share at least one real club (`clubmembers:*`) with them (plus themselves); admin still sees everyone; name-only guests still get global anonymous counts and no names. Tested locally with 3 members (A+B Cuisine, C Field): A saw A+B, C saw only C, guest got counts only.

**2026-10-02 (Bug: public club descriptions missing on 3 of 4 cards) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Cause: I'd added a 2nd param `hideDesc` to `clubCardHtml(c, hideDesc)` earlier today and the public grid still called `clubsForGrid().map(clubCardHtml)` — `Array.map` passes the INDEX as the 2nd argument, so every card after the first (index>0 = truthy) hid its description. Fixed: all callers now use `.map(c => clubCardHtml(c, false))`. Lesson: never pass a multi-arg function straight to `.map`.

**2026-10-02 (Members can change their own PIN) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: "add a button where you're able to change your pin." New "🔑 Change my PIN" button on the signed-in Clubs view (both the club-picker screen and the dashboard) opening a popup: current PIN, new PIN, new PIN again. **Backend (`PUT /api/users/:id`, which already accepted `newPin` for the forced first-sign-in change):** a voluntary change now requires `currentPin` — otherwise anyone holding an unlocked, still-signed-in phone (sessions last 180 days) could change the PIN and lock the owner out. Those current-PIN guesses count against the same per-username hourly counter as `/api/login` (10/hr), so this route can't be used to brute-force the 4-digit PIN around the login rate limit; once tripped, even the correct current PIN is refused until the hour resets. The forced first-login flow (admin-created accounts on PIN 1234, `mustChangePin` true) is unchanged and still needs no current PIN. Other devices stay signed in after a change (session tokens aren't revoked) — not asked for, flagging. **Tested (local worker):** no/wrong current PIN -> 401 "Current PIN is incorrect"; bad new-PIN format -> 400; correct change -> old PIN login rejected, new PIN login works; no token -> 401; 12 rapid wrong attempts -> rate limited, and the correct current PIN also refused afterward; forced-change flow still works without a current PIN. **UI:** popup validation messages (empty, not 4 digits, mismatch, same as current, wrong current) and the success path, popup auto-closes. Fields are explicit 16px so iPhones don't zoom on tap. Not tested on a real phone.

**2026-10-01 (Fix: stale production KV club roster; availability is now one general record per person, not per club) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Two things. (1) **Real production bug, caught during deploy verification of the Quest rename:** `GET /api/clubs` and the Quest rename's link preview were both still showing "Active Gaming" live, even though `data/clubs.json` on GitHub had "Quest" and the deploy had gone out. Root cause: the Club Manager's `clubRoster` KV mirror only ever gets refreshed by the admin panel's own write path (`writeClubRoster()`, called from the `/api/admin/clubs/*` routes) — a direct `git commit` to `data/clubs.json` (which is what both the Quest rename and this session's earlier edits were) updates the GitHub source file but never touches the KV mirror, so `GET /api/clubs` kept serving the stale cached roster from the last admin-panel edit. Fixed immediately by writing the correct current roster into the real production KV `clubRoster` key directly (`wrangler kv key put --remote`, verified this session's `wrangler` is authenticated to the real Cloudflare account, not just local dev) and confirmed live. **Flagging clearly: this will recur for any future direct-to-`data/clubs.json` git edit** (by me or another agent) — only edits made *through* the admin panel keep KV in sync automatically; a raw file edit needs this same manual KV fix afterward until someone builds an auto-sync (e.g. a scheduled job that just re-derives KV from GitHub, or making `GET /api/clubs` compare freshness) — not attempted here since it's out of scope for a same-day fix. (2) **Lujane, immediately after seeing the "which club are you in" picker land:** "it should be general availability not a different one for each club." Correct catch — until now, "Add your availability" was silently a *separate* record per club (`ideaavail:{clubId}:{personId}`), so a member in 3 clubs had to re-enter the same Tuesday-evening availability 3 times, once per club's idea-board page, with no indication they were different records. Redesigned to one record per person, period: `ideaAvailKey(personId)` (no clubId), the routes moved from `/api/clubs/:clubId/idea-availability(/all)` to global `/api/idea-availability(/all)` (and the admin names-only mirror similarly to `/api/admin/idea-availability`, no clubId), and `readIdeaAvailPeople()` now scans all `ideaavail:` keys instead of a per-club-prefixed subset — so "Everyone's availability" is now also one genuinely community-wide aggregate, identical no matter which club's page you view it from, not a separate headcount per club. On the frontend, `openClubIdeas()` (switching which club's board you're looking at) no longer resets or reloads the availability state at all — it's correct and current regardless of which club page it's rendered on, confirmed by testing with zero refetch when switching clubs. Added a one-line hint under "Add your availability" explaining this ("your one general availability — the same everywhere, not a separate one per club") so it doesn't look like a mistake. **Tested:** `node --check`/script-parse before and after both changes; local `wrangler dev` — set availability as a guest while viewing Cuisine's board, switched to Arts & Crafts' board, and the identical grid appeared with zero network refetch (`ideaAvailLoading` stayed false); confirmed "Everyone's availability" aggregates respondents globally (2 different test guests, both counted, regardless of which club page each had set theirs from) — re-verified the exact bug class from yesterday (the "stuck on a stale error forever" pattern) doesn't reappear with the new global-key guards. **Not done:** no migration of any *pre-existing* per-club availability data that might already be sitting in KV under the old `ideaavail:{clubId}:{personId}` keys from before today — those old records are now orphaned (never read by the new code) rather than merged into the new global key; given the system only shipped in the last two days, the realistic blast radius is small, but flagging it rather than silently leaving it unmentioned.

**2026-10-01 ("Which club are you in now?" picker for signed-in Clubs tab; Active Gaming renamed to Quest) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `data/clubs.json`** — Lujane: "when u log in [to] clubs i dont want the four clubs to show like that, i want it to ask which club are you in now? and then they can press one or multiple and edit their choice later. and then all the bubble vote and availability would show." For a signed-in member (or the site admin), the Clubs tab no longer shows the plain browse-all-4-clubs grid. Instead it asks "Which club are you in now?" — a multi-select list of all clubs (checkboxes, pre-checked from the member's actual admin-assigned clubs as a sensible default) — Save stores the pick in `localStorage` (`cmMyClubPicks`, a personal view preference, independent of actual admin-assigned membership) and an "✏️ Edit which club(s) you're in" link reopens the same picker anytime with the current picks still checked. Once set: only the picked clubs' cards show (tapping one still opens that club's full board + availability grid, completely unchanged) plus a combined, fully-interactive vote feed scoped to just those clubs underneath — reused the exact same combined-feed code built yesterday for anonymous visitors (`publicIdeasLoadAll`, now takes an optional club list; `publicIdeaBubbleHtml`; `renderPublicIdeasFeedHtml`, now takes a title/hint/empty-message so the copy fits "your clubs" instead of "everyone") rather than writing a second implementation — voting on this scoped feed just works with zero gate changes needed, since `publicIdeaCanInteract()` already returns true for any signed-in session. Didn't attempt an inline multi-club availability grid (would mean converting ~15 functions and a dozen state fields from single-club-global to per-club-keyed) — tapping into a picked club's card for availability, which already works perfectly, covers that without the risk of a big availability-system refactor; flagging this scoping choice clearly rather than silently doing less than asked. Also renamed Active Gaming → **Quest** with a reworded description mentioning "thrilling activities" per request — `id`/`slug` (`club-activegaming`/`active-gaming`) kept immutable as always, so the existing shared link `4dasistas.ca/clubs/active-gaming` and any data tied to the id are unaffected, only the display title/desc changed. **Tested:** `node -e` script-parse before/after; local `wrangler dev` — signed up a real test member, confirmed the picker pre-checks their actual club, multi-selected a second club and saved, confirmed only those two clubs' cards + a combined feed scoped to just them render (seeded real ideas in a picked and a non-picked club and confirmed the non-picked one's idea never appears); "Edit which club(s)" reopens the picker with prior picks intact; a 0-selection Save is blocked with an inline error; tapping a picked club's card still opens its full existing board with the untouched 15-day availability grid. **Not tested:** the exact same code path as the site admin specifically (vs. a member) — couldn't get a real local admin cookie through the browser test harness this session, but it's the identical `renderMyClubsSectionHtml()` call regardless of which flag makes the view non-anonymous, so this isn't believed to be a real gap.

**2026-10-01 (Security: rate limit on /api/login) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane asked for a general safety check after today's auth changes. Found one real gap worth fixing: `/api/login` had no rate limiting at all, while `/api/signup` already did. This mattered more than it used to — usernames went from an arbitrary self-chosen string to a deterministic `firstname.initial` derived from the member's own name (easily guessable from names already visible on club rosters/idea boards), and PINs are only 4 digits (10,000 combinations) — without a cap, an attacker could script unlimited guesses against a known username and brute-force the PIN in minutes. Added two KV-backed hourly counters, same `expirationTtl` pattern already used for signup rate-limiting: one per IP (20/hr, stops one attacker hammering many accounts) and one per *username* (10/hr, stops a botnet spreading attempts across many IPs at a single account). Every attempt increments the counter — success or failure — so the limit can't be probed around, and once a username's limit trips, even the correct PIN is blocked until the hour resets (by design, not a bug) rather than leaking a signal distinguishing "rate limited" from "about to succeed." **Tested:** `node --check`; local `wrangler dev` — created a real test account, confirmed a normal correct login works, then fired 11 bad-PIN attempts at the same username and confirmed attempts 10+ were rejected with the rate-limit message (not "incorrect"), confirmed a subsequent attempt with the *correct* PIN was also blocked (closes the bypass), and confirmed a completely different username logged in normally and was unaffected — the limit is per-account, not global.

**2026-10-01 (Fix: WhatsApp button unreachable on iPhone home-screen app; quick-login now has its own shareable URL) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Two quick fixes. (1) Lujane: "i made it as an app on hoemscrene so the green button oin top right of screen cannot be touched on ipohoen 17." The fixed WhatsApp join button (`.wa-fixed-btn`, top:14px) never accounted for `env(safe-area-inset-top)` the way the page `header` and a few overlays (`.av-exit`, `.update-banner`) already do. In a normal Safari tab that's invisible — the browser's own address bar sits above it. But this site is a PWA added to the home screen (`"display":"standalone"`, `viewport-fit=cover`, `apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style: black-translucent` — all already set up correctly), so in that mode the page draws all the way under the status bar/Dynamic Island with nothing else above it, and a flat `top:14px` landed the button directly under/behind the Dynamic Island on an iPhone 17 — unreachable. Fixed with the same `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px))` pattern already proven elsewhere in this file. (2) Lujane: "when i press sign up and already filled the form but need an account, it takes me to it but ti doesnt have its own url so plz fix that" — the "Already filled out a form? Just set up your login" quick-login toggle (shipped yesterday) only flipped a `state.fallQuickLogin` boolean, so the URL stayed `#/clubs/join` whichever form was showing — not shareable/bookmarkable on its own. Added a dedicated `#/clubs/quicklogin` route: `currentSlugParts()` emits it when `fallQuickLogin` is true, and `applySlugParts()` recognizes it on load/back-forward (and explicitly clears the flag when landing on the plain `/clubs/join` slug, so the two states can't bleed into each other). **Tested:** `node -e` script-parse before/after; local static-serve click-through — toggling the sign-up page updates the address bar between `#/clubs/join` and `#/clubs/quicklogin` live; a fresh page load at `#/clubs/quicklogin` lands directly on the compact name+PIN form; a fresh load at `#/clubs/join` is unaffected, still the full form. The iPhone status-bar fix could not be verified on an actual device/Dynamic Island from this environment — it's the identical, already-working pattern used by other fixed-position elements in this same file, not a new technique.

**2026-10-01 (Fix: login broken for every account created before today's username changes) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: "i tried to sign in as user put it says incoreect, see for past users dont change the structure of their usernamer, onyl nwo moving forward." Real, serious bug from the username-format churn yesterday: `sanitizeUsername()` — used to validate the username typed into `/api/login` (and the admin's manual username edit) — had been tightened to strictly require the new `firstname.initial` shape (`/^[a-z]+\.[a-z][a-z0-9]*$/`). Anyone whose account predates that change (an old free-text username like `sister_amina23`, or one from the brief phone-number-only period) now fails that regex and gets rejected before the code even looks their username up in the database — their account was never touched, they just couldn't get past the format check to log in. Fixed by making `sanitizeUsername()` permissive again (`/^[a-z0-9._-]{2,40}$/` — normalize case/whitespace only, don't enforce a shape) since it's only ever used to validate an *existing* username being looked up, never to decide what a new one looks like. New accounts are completely unaffected — `generateUniqueUsername()`/`usernameBaseFromName()` (the actual firstname.initial generator) never called `sanitizeUsername()` in the first place, so it keeps producing `amina.k`-style usernames for every signup from here on, exactly as intended. **Tested:** `node --check`; local `wrangler dev` — seeded a KV user record by hand to simulate a real pre-existing account with an old-style username (`sister_amina23`) and confirmed it now logs in successfully; confirmed a brand-new signup still gets a clean `firstname.initial` username (`new.q` from "New Style Q.") exactly as before. **Action for Lujane:** if any real members already tried and failed to log in during the window this bug was live, they can just try again now — nothing about their account needs fixing, the username in the database was never wrong.

**2026-10-01 (Availability grid extended 10→15 days) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: "make sure all the schedule stuff isnt messed up, there shouldnt duplicates and should be user friednly for phoen users, and ability to see next 15 days." Audited the two things that plausibly qualify as "schedule stuff": (1) the idea-board availability grids ("Add your availability" / "Everyone's availability", currently a 10-day/2-row view), and (2) the main community Calendar's recurring-event expansion (`occurrencesInMonth()`) and the `ALL_ITEMS` data merge — checked the real `data/*.json` files for duplicate ids (none found) and re-read the recurring-event expansion logic, which already de-dupes a single item's dates within a month via `[...new Set(...)]`; found no actual duplicate-rendering bug in the calendar. The concrete, actionable part of the ask — "ability to see next 15 days" — maps directly onto the availability grid, so extended it: `iavNext10Dates()` → `iavNext15Dates()` (still starting 4 days out, per the existing "never today/next 3 days" rule), and both `iavGridRowsHtml()` (personal grid) and `eavGridHtml()` (everyone's grid) now split into three 5-day rows instead of two, stacking vertically exactly like the existing two rows did — no CSS changes needed since `.iav-grid-rows` is just an unbounded flex column and each `.iav-grid`/`.eav-grid` row is already a self-contained 5-column grid. **Tested:** `node -e` script-parse before/after; a standalone Node check confirmed `iavNext15Dates()` produces exactly 15 unique sequential dates with zero duplicates and splits cleanly into three 5-date rows; local `wrangler dev` + browser — the live rendered grid showed exactly 15 unique day labels (Mon 5 → Mon 19) with `new Set(labels).size === 15`; tapped a cell in the third (newly added) row and confirmed it set availability for exactly that one date, nothing else; counted `.iav-cell` elements (45 = 15 days × 3 time-of-day parts, as expected) to confirm no stray/duplicate cells. No markup/CSS bug was found or needed fixing — this was a pure capacity increase on an already-correct grid.

**2026-09-30 ("Already filled out a form" quick-login option on sign-up) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: "in sign up, put an option for already filled a form and just need a username and pin? and allow users to do exactly that, then they can log in normally." Someone who already told us their club picks some other way (DM, WhatsApp, paper) but never got a login shouldn't have to redo the whole multi-step Fall sign-up form just to get credentials. Added a toggle link at the top of the sign-up page ("Already filled out a form? Just set up your login →") that swaps the full form for a tiny one: name + PIN only, no club picks, no WhatsApp number, no terms checkbox. **Backend:** `clubId` on `POST /api/signup` is now optional — when omitted, the account is created with zero club memberships and an empty waitlist (skips the roster-membership check and the per-club member-list write entirely); an admin assigns their club(s) afterward from the existing member-edit checkboxes, same as any admin-created profile. Passing a real `clubId` still works exactly as before (the main Fall form is unaffected). **Tested:** `node --check`/script-parse before and after; local `wrangler dev` — signup with no `clubId` returns `clubs:[]` and a normal working login; signup with a valid `clubId` unaffected; signup with a bogus `clubId` still correctly rejected ("Please pick a club from the list"); full in-browser click-through — toggled to the quick form, filled "Browser Test Z." + a PIN, submitted, landed back on Clubs signed in with the "an organizer will add you to your club(s)" notice.

**2026-09-30 (Public Clubs tab redesign: Log In/Sign Up buttons, Most Popular Clubs row, combined vote-only idea feed) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: "when anyone click clubs, for public view it should show on right button sign up, left button log in, then under that it should show 4 horizontal panels of the clubs with their desc and logo, and it above it a title MOST POPULAR CLUBS and then under it should show ALL the bubble vote events from all the clubs, with the info button and the google map button. public users shouldnt be able to add or vote but they can see, and it would prompt them to sign up or log in if they want to add/vote." Scoped this entirely to the logged-out/anonymous view (`const anon = !getCmSession() && state.cmAdminLoggedIn !== true`) — a signed-in member or the site admin still sees exactly today's Clubs tab (own panels/admin tools), nothing changed for them. For an anonymous visitor: (1) the old stacked "JOIN A CLUB! / Already a member? Log in" became two side-by-side buttons, Log In on the left and Sign Up on the right (same underlying actions — `clubsLoginOpenBtn` toggle and the existing `data-hometab="fallsignup"` flow — just relabeled/relaid-out). (2) New "MOST POPULAR CLUBS" heading above the club cards, and the cards (extracted into a shared `clubCardHtml()` so both layouts use the same markup) now render in a new `.clubs-grid-row` — a horizontally-scrollable flex row (4 cards at ~200px each) instead of the 2-column wrapping grid, giving the "4 horizontal panels" layout on any screen width. (3) New combined idea-bubble feed below that ("WHAT EVERYONE'S VOTING ON") — `publicIdeasLoadAll()` fetches every real club's `/ideas` in parallel and merges them (each idea tagged with its club for a small 🏷 label on the bubble), reusing the exact same bubble/info-popup/map-button visuals as the per-club idea boards (`publicIdeaBubbleHtml`/`openPublicIdeaInfo`, new functions parallel to the existing `ideaBubbleHtml`/`openIdeaInfo` but reading from the merged list and deliberately dropping the admin edit/delete affordances, which don't make sense in a mixed-club view). (4) Gating: unlike the per-club idea board (which still offers name-only guest voting, unchanged), this combined feed requires a real account — tapping a bubble to vote, with no member session and not admin, opens a small popup ("Want to vote or add an idea? Sign up or log in first") with Log In / Sign Up buttons instead of silently failing or asking for just a name; the info (i) and map (📍) buttons work for everyone with no gate, matching "public users... can see" exactly. **Tested:** `node -e` script-parse before/after; local `wrangler dev` with two real test ideas created in two different clubs (via guest headers, to seed the feed) — confirmed both appear correctly tagged and sorted by votes in the combined feed on a static-served copy of the frontend (fetch calls redirected to the local Worker for this test); tapping a bubble while logged out correctly opened the sign-up/login prompt and did NOT vote; the prompt's Log In button correctly opened the existing login picker and its Sign Up button correctly navigated to the fall sign-up form; the info button opened its popup normally (no gate); signed up a fresh test account mid-session and confirmed the Clubs tab reverted to exactly today's logged-in member view (no public buttons/title/feed) — the anon-only scoping works; fixed a visual mismatch where the new Log In button inherited an old unrelated `.clubs-top-actions` square-button CSS rule and looked boxy next to Sign Up, overrode it inline to match. **Not done:** haven't tested actually voting as a real logged-in member from inside this specific combined view end-to-end against production (the vote call itself reuses the existing, already-proven per-club vote endpoint and headers, just targeting each idea's own `_clubId` — logic verified by reading, not a live click-through with a real session in this exact screen).

**2026-09-30 (Usernames changed again: firstname.lastinitial, not phone numbers) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane, right after the phone-number change above shipped: "wait no usernames should be first name then . then first letter of the initial." Reverted the phone-number scheme entirely. Usernames are now auto-generated server-side from the name already being collected — "Amina K." → `amina.k` — nobody types or picks a username at all anymore, anywhere. Collisions get a numeric suffix (a second "Amina K." becomes `amina.k2`). Removed the now-redundant username input from the fall sign-up form, the admin "Create a profile" form, and the admin "Create Member" form — all three now take just a name and derive the login automatically; the sign-up and create-profile success messages/welcome banners show the real generated username so it can be written down. The admin's "typing an existing username to add someone to another club" shortcut in the Create Member/Create-a-profile forms is gone (nothing is typed anymore, so there's nothing to match against) — added a `existingUserId` path to the same endpoint for the "tick several clubs at once" admin flow to keep working internally, and pointed anyone wanting to add an already-existing member to a club at the member edit screen's per-club checkboxes, which already does exactly that. The one place someone still types a username is logging in (member login screens) and the admin's edit-member field (for fixing a typo/collision) — both relabeled back from "Phone number" to "Username" with a `firstname.initial` example. **Tested:** `node --check`/script-parse before and after; local `wrangler dev` — signing up "Amina K." produces `amina.k`, signing up "Amina Karim" right after produces `amina.k2` (collision suffix works), logging in with each separately-generated username+PIN works; admin create-member and create-profile both tested end-to-end including the multi-club "create in club A, reuse the same new profile's id in club B" flow, confirmed `addedExisting:true` on the second call with the member correctly showing up in both clubs.

**2026-09-30 (New site icon) — Claude (chat) — `assets/icon-192.png`, `assets/icon-512.png`, `assets/apple-touch-icon.png`** — Lujane sent a new square pink/cream "4PS" logo mark and asked to make it the site's icon everywhere. Resized it to each file's exact required dimensions (192, 512, 180) with `sips` and replaced the three existing icon files in place — no code changes needed since `index.html`/`manifest.webmanifest` already point at these exact paths. Deliberately left `assets/OFFICIAL LOGO.png` (the wide social-share og:image banner) untouched — that's a different-shaped asset for a different purpose, not "the icon." **Tested:** verified output dimensions with `sips -g pixelWidth -g pixelHeight`; visually checked the 512px version has generous padding around the mark so it won't get clipped by Android's circular adaptive-icon mask or iOS's rounded-corner mask (the exact problem that bit the previous icon, per the 2026-09-28 log entry below).

**2026-09-30 (Club logins are now phone numbers, not free-text usernames; fixed club logo upload always failing) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Two separate requests. (1) Lujane: "for profiles, make all usernames a phone number not anything else." The global member login (used by the fall sign-up form, the Clubs-tab member login, the per-club members login, and the admin's Create-a-profile / Create-Member / Edit-member tools) used a free-text username (3-20 letters/numbers/underscore). Changed the one shared `sanitizeUsername()` in the Worker to require a phone number instead: strips all non-digit formatting (spaces, dashes, parens, a leading +), also strips a leading North American country code so "+1 416 555 1234", "(416) 555-1234" and "416-555-1234" all normalize to the same stored value and can't accidentally create duplicate accounts for the same person. Every user-facing "username" label, placeholder, hint and error message across the sign-up form, both member-login screens, and the admin member tools now says "phone number"; inputs switched to `type="tel"` for the right mobile keyboard. The "@username" handle-style display in member lists was also dropped since a bare phone number isn't a handle. Old-style alpha usernames are now correctly rejected everywhere a phone number is required. (2) Lujane: "why does it say img too large or not supported in club admin editing, it shouldnt do that." Real bug: the club logo picker (`clubDirLogoChange`, added in yesterday's Club Directory admin feature) uploaded the raw file as a base64 data URL with only a client-side 4MB raw-file-size gate — but the server's `sanitizePhoto()` caps the base64 string at ~225KB, which any normal phone photo (typically 1-4MB raw) blows through easily, guaranteeing the "Logo image is too large or not a supported format" error on basically every real upload. Every *other* photo picker in the app (member profile photo, resource image) already downscales to a small square and re-compresses as JPEG via canvas before sending — the club logo picker was the only one skipping that step. Fixed by applying the same pattern (300x300, JPEG quality 0.8) and raised the raw-file gate to 10MB since the real cap is now the compression step, not the raw upload size. **Tested:** `node --check`/script-parse before and after; local `wrangler dev` — `/api/signup`, `/api/login` and `/api/admin/club-members` all now reject a letters-based username with "Please enter a valid phone number" and correctly accept/normalize `416-555-1234`, `(416) 555-1234` and `+1 416 555 1234` to the identical stored value, confirmed logging in with one format after signing up with a differently-formatted version of the same number works; logo fix verified by code-reading against the working member/resource photo pickers (same exact pattern, just different crop size) — did not have a real image file on hand in this environment to click through the picker live, but the size-math is the direct, confirmed cause (4MB raw ≈ 5.3MB base64 vs the server's 225KB cap) so this is a structural fix, not a guess.

**2026-09-30 (Fix: guest availability permanently stuck on an error; admin view still showed Join/member-login buttons) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: "things are really ruined. multiple login buttons, ppl not able to put their availability." Two real, confirmed bugs, both reproduced locally before fixing: (1) **Availability stuck broken for every new guest.** `wireIdeaAvailGrid()` (wired on every render of a club's idea-board page, added when the two-row-grid/Edit-gate feature shipped earlier today) called `ideaAvailLoad()` unconditionally whenever `state.ideaAvail === null`, including on the very first render before a visitor has entered a name. With no guest/member identity yet, the Worker correctly 401s with "Enter your name first" — but that message got written into `state.ideaAvailError`, and the retry guard on the same line required `!state.ideaAvailError`, so it would never try again. The guest-join handler already reset `ideaAvail`/`ideaAvailLoadedFor` after someone typed their name and joined, but never cleared `ideaAvailError` — so the stale 401 silently blocked every subsequent attempt, permanently, for the rest of that page view. Nobody could ever add their availability after this shipped. Fixed two ways: `wireIdeaAvailGrid()` now only attempts the load when `ideaMe()` is truthy (matches what `everyoneAvailLoad()` already did correctly), so the doomed pre-identity request never fires at all; and the join handler now also resets `ideaAvailError = ''` as defense in depth. (2) **"Multiple login buttons":** the Clubs tab's "JOIN A CLUB!" / "Already a member? Log in" block only hid itself for a logged-in club *member* (`getCmSession()`), not for the logged-in site *admin* — so Lujane, logged in as admin, saw her own admin panel at the bottom of the page while the Join/member-login buttons stayed stuck at the top, reading as duplicate/conflicting login prompts. Swapped the check to the existing `isAdminViewer()` helper (already used elsewhere for exactly "member session or admin session," just not here) — no new logic needed, one word changed. **Tested:** `node -e` parse of both script blocks before/after; locally reproduced both bugs first (confirmed on the live site via browser automation — joined as a guest and watched "Add your availability" stay stuck on the red error after naming myself; forced `cmAdminLoggedIn=true` and confirmed Join/Login buttons stayed visible), then re-tested against the fixed file with a stubbed network layer simulating the exact 401-then-200 sequence a real guest hits, confirming the grid now renders after joining; separately confirmed admin-logged-in now hides Join/Login while a logged-out visitor still sees them normally. **Not yet verified against production** — about to push and will check the live site immediately after.

**2026-09-30 (Club Manager rebuild: single source of truth for club identity, KV-mirrored roster, immutable slugs) — Claude (chat) — `data/clubs.json`, `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — After fixing the deleted-Theatre bug earlier today by hand-removing it from four hardcoded lists, Lujane confirmed she wants the real fix: "yes rebuild." The underlying problem: club identity was split across `data/clubs.json` (what the admin CRUD actually edits) and four hardcoded lists that nothing kept in sync — `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS`/`CLUB_SLUGS`/`clubColors` (index.html) and `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS`/`CLUB_META` (worker.js). Any admin add/delete only touched `data/clubs.json`, so the other four silently kept stale or deleted clubs "alive" until someone noticed and patched all four by hand (happened twice: club-adhd, then club-theater). **Fix:** `data/clubs.json` is now the only place club identity lives, mirrored into a new KV key (`clubRoster`) so reads don't need a full site rebuild to see admin changes. All five old hardcoded lists are gone. Each club now carries an explicit, immutable `slug` (set once at creation via the existing `slugify()`, never recomputed — verified it reproduces all 5 clubs' real historical slugs, including the non-derivable "Field" title → "field" slug), plus new `emoji` and `colour` fields (the admin editor got matching inputs). **Worker:** `readClubRoster()`/`writeClubRoster()` (KV-first, falls back to a GitHub read and backfills KV if empty), `isKnownClubId()`, `sanitizeColour()`. New public `GET /api/clubs` returns the live roster. `ideaClub()` is now async and checks the live roster (6 call sites updated). `/api/signup`'s id/waitlist checks now read the roster instead of `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS`. The `/clubs/:slug` link-preview route resolves against the roster instead of `CLUB_META`. `POST/PUT/DELETE /api/admin/clubs` all write to GitHub first (unchanged, still the durable source) and only mirror into KV after a successful commit; `PUT` keeps `slug` immutable by deleting it from the incoming fields before merging. **Frontend:** `clubCol(id)` replaces the `clubColors` lookup (reads `.colour` off `clubsData`, falls back to the old charcoal default for any club missing one); `clubsForGrid()` is now just `clubsData` (no more re-adding "missing" hardcoded entries — this was the actual root cause of the Theatre bug); `clubSlug`/`clubIdFromSlug` read the `slug` field directly; new `fetchClubsLive()` tries `/api/clubs` first and falls back to the static `data/clubs.json` fetch, wired into `loadData()`. **Caught before shipping:** an Edit-tool escaping artifact left a literal `\\'` (not a proper escaped apostrophe) in two success-alert strings in `clubDirSave()`/`clubDirDelete()`, which broke the whole inline script — `node -e` parse of both script blocks failed with `SyntaxError: missing ) after argument list`. Reworded both messages to avoid the apostrophe instead of re-escaping it; re-parsed clean. **Tested:** `node --check` on worker.js + a `new Function()` parse of both index.html script blocks, both clean. Local `wrangler dev --local` with the KV seeded from `data/clubs.json`: `GET /api/clubs` returns the roster correctly; `club-cuisine` (real) passes the idea-board/ideas-list gate with 200, `club-theater` (deleted) correctly falls through to the 404 catch-all on both routes — confirms gating now comes from the live roster, not a hardcoded list; `/clubs/arts-crafts` and `/clubs/field` both resolve correct per-club link-preview meta tags from the roster. Logged in as admin locally and hit `POST /api/admin/clubs` — failed with "clubs.json not found" as expected (no real `GITHUB_TOKEN` available in this environment), which confirms the route reaches the GitHub-read step and fails gracefully rather than crashing or writing a corrupt roster; the actual GitHub-write + KV-mirror path can only be exercised with the real production token, so that specific step is verified by code reading against the existing working CRUD pattern, not by a live run. Frontend served statically (no worker) at `/clubs` — confirmed `fetchClubsLive()` falls back to `data/clubs.json` cleanly with a clear console warning when `/api/clubs` 404s, all 5 clubs render (Theatre absent) with each card's border colour exactly matching its `colour` field (e.g. `#e1eaf1` → `rgb(225,234,241)`), and `clubSlug('club-retreats')`/`clubIdFromSlug('field')` round-trip correctly through the new explicit `slug` field. **Not done:** have not exercised a real end-to-end admin create/edit/delete against production GitHub credentials (not available locally) — first live use should be watched for anything unexpected around the GitHub-write-then-KV-mirror sequence, though the code path matches the existing working CRUD exactly plus one extra KV write. Also have not revisited the other known-but-deferred items (rate-limiting admin login, locking down `GET /api/clubs` from scraping) — not asked for, not touched.

**2026-09-30 (Fix: deleted Theatre club still showing; idea-board layout order; 4-day availability window; one green for all time slots) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Four follow-ups. (1) **"I deleted Theatre in admin but it's still there"**: real bug, root-caused. Club identity is split across `data/clubs.json` (what an admin-facing club editor — built by another agent, not me — actually deletes from) AND four separate hardcoded lists that nothing keeps in sync: `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS`/`CLUB_SLUGS`/`clubColors` (index.html) and `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS`/`CLUB_META` (worker.js). `clubsForGrid()` merges `clubsData` with any `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS` entry NOT already in `clubsData` — so deleting a club from `data/clubs.json` alone makes `clubsForGrid()` treat it as "missing" and silently re-add it. Confirmed `club-theater` was correctly gone from `data/clubs.json` but still hardcoded in all four other places; removed it from all of them (same pattern as retiring club-adhd on 2026-09-28). This is the same fragmentation problem flagged four days ago as needing a real Club Manager refactor — still not built; every admin deletion through whatever tool touches only `data/clubs.json` will keep silently failing to fully take effect until that's consolidated. **Flagging this clearly: if she deletes another club, it needs the same manual hardcoded-list cleanup from me (or Kilo/Cline) unless the Club Manager gets built.** (2) **Idea-board page order**: the "Add option" form now sits under "Add your availability" (moved `addForm` + `ideasStatus` down); the idea bubbles stay where they were, right under the join/who block. (3) **Availability window**: `iavNext10Dates()` now starts 4 days out instead of today (today/tomorrow/+2/+3 never shown) — same rule as `minNewEventDate()` for new events, and since `eavGridHtml()` ("Everyone's availability") reuses the same function, both grids shifted together automatically. (4) **One colour for every time slot**: `.iav-cell.on.iav-c-morning/afternoon/evening` (yellow/orange/blue) collapsed into a single `.iav-cell.on` rule using the same green as "available all day" — tapping Morning, Afternoon or Evening all just turn green now, no more per-part colour-coding. **Tested**: script-parse before/after; in-browser — Theatre confirmed gone from the clubs grid after the fix; layout order verified via DOM child order (title→link→sub→who→bubbles→availability→add-form→status→everyone's-availability); date range confirmed starting exactly 4 days out; tapped three different time-of-day cells on three different dates and confirmed identical computed background colour on all three (a first attempt at this last check used `.click()` instead of dispatching a real PointerEvent and wrongly looked like nothing happened — re-tested correctly before concluding it worked).

**2026-09-29 (Clubs admin: fix "Club not found" - data/clubs.json is {items:[...]}, not a bare array) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: "when i try to delet club ins 4ds it shos Club not found." Root cause: the clubs admin endpoints (added earlier today) assumed `data/clubs.json` was a plain array; it's actually `{items:[...]}`. `Array.isArray(file.content)` was silently false on every request, so `clubs` resolved to `[]` every time - GET returned an empty list, PUT/DELETE always 404'd regardless of id. POST would have written a bare array back (discarding the `{items}` wrapper) but never actually ran - checked `git log -- data/clubs.json`, no commit from these endpoints exists, so nothing on disk got corrupted. All four handlers now read `file.content.items` (falling back to a bare array if the shape ever changes) and write `{items: updated}` back. **Tested:** `node --check`; confirmed via `git log` that no prior add/edit/delete attempt had actually committed a corrupted shape. **Not independently tested end-to-end** (would need a real admin session cookie, not available from this environment) - fix verified by re-reading the actual `data/clubs.json` on disk and tracing it against the exact code path, not just inferred; flag if delete still fails after this deploy.

Log in" text link above the JOIN A CLUB! button (`#clubsLoginOpenBtn` — the click handler for this id already existed from before the Log In button was removed, so this is markup-only, no new JS). **2026-09-26 (Fix search bar: space bar unusable, laggy typing, results vanishing on mobile) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane reported that on mobile, the search bar wouldn't let her type a space, got laggy, and "the actual full calendar disappears." Traced all three to the same `input` handler on `#globalSearch` (plus one compounding bug):
1. It stored `e.target.value.trim()` into `state.searchQuery`, and `render()` (called after every keystroke) unconditionally wrote that trimmed value straight back into the input's `.value` - a typed trailing space got trimmed and rewritten out before the next frame, so the space bar visually never registered anything. This is 100% reproducible and not actually mobile-specific, just far more noticeable there.
2. That same `render()` redraws the *entire* page (calendar grid, filters, directory view, etc.), not just the search results - doing that synchronously on every keystroke is what caused the lag, and on slower mobile hardware likely what made the calendar look like it was flickering/disappearing mid-type.
3. Separately, `normalizeSearchText()` didn't collapse/trim whitespace, so even a legitimate trailing space (had it survived bug #1) made the substring match fail against almost everything - "pickle " matches nothing where "pickle" matched several events. This is probably the literal "calendar disappears" moment: results collapse to zero the instant a stray space lands in the query.

**Fix:** keep the raw untrimmed value in `state.searchQuery` (trimming now happens only inside `normalizeSearchText()`, for matching, never touching what's in the input); `normalizeSearchText()` now collapses whitespace runs and trims; the full `render()` is debounced 200ms after typing stops (`renderSearchSuggestions()`, the actual live dropdown, still updates immediately since it's cheap); `render()` only reassigns the search input's `.value` when it's actually out of sync with state instead of unconditionally every call. Also grepped for the same "input listener → full render() every keystroke" pattern elsewhere in the file - found none (the only other two `input` listeners are lightweight targeted DOM updates, not full re-renders).

**Tested:** `node --check` on the extracted inline script before and after; re-ran `build_content.py` + `generate_calendar.py` before deploying (2 more concurrent admin-editor commits landed mid-fix - "Table Talks", edited "Dawah Training" - neither touched `index.html`, rebased clean); grepped the live served `index.html` post-deploy for the new `searchRenderTimer` variable to confirm the upload actually landed (learned last session that "No updated asset files to upload" or an immediate post-deploy curl can read stale/unchanged for a few seconds even on a real successful deploy - waited it out and reconfirmed rather than trusting the first check). **Not tested**: haven't used a real phone to confirm the lag/disappearing is actually gone - the root cause (unconditional full-page re-render synchronously on every keystroke, on top of a value being force-reset into the input every time) is a solid, sufficient explanation for exactly these three symptoms together, but worth Lujane confirming on her device.

Fixed all of them to `sportKey: "pickleball"`, using `alsoSportKeys` for the one that's genuinely also a tennis event (the documented pattern for multi-sport events - it just wasn't being used). `autoEmoji()` now normalizes case before the `SPORT_EMOJI` lookup as a defensive fix. **Prevention**: the Sport field in both the in-page admin editor and the Decap CMS (`admin/config.yml`) is now a `select` dropdown of canonical sport keys instead of free text - existing non-canonical values are preserved as an extra option rather than silently cleared on save. Trips globe ("Trips on the globe" side list): was rendered in whatever order `data/trips.json` happened to be in - now sorted earliest→latest (latest-first when viewing past), undated trips pushed to the end. Added a continent filter (Africa/Asia/Europe/North America/South America/Other), derived from a title+location regex classifier since no continent field exists in the data - only shows when there's more than one continent to pick between. The globe itself now plots only the filtered+sorted set. A new "Patagonia" trip landed mid-session; added South America to the classifier for it.

**Hit two more concurrent-edit races** on top of the ones logged below: "Create 'Pickle & paddle'" and "Create 'Patagonia'" both landed via the live site's own admin editor while this session was working. Neither touched files this session had modified, so both rebased clean - no manual conflict resolution needed beyond the routine `calendar.ics` regen conflict (took the freshest regen each time, as always).
