Per a follow-up ask, laid out as two stacked 5-day grids (not one 10-day scrolling row) for iPhone. **Also fixed in passing, unrelated to the above:** `wrangler deploy` was uploading `.wrangler/`'s local Miniflare dev-state (KV/R2/cache SQLite files from local testing) as public static assets on the live site — `.assetsignore` never excluded it; added the exclusion and redeployed to purge the already-live stray files (verified gone via curl). **Hit a rebase**: another agent's automated `calendar.ics` regen landed on `main` between my pull and push — rebased cleanly, took the freshest regen for the one conflicting file (pure generated content, no hand-edits lost). **Tested:** JS/worker syntax-checked with `node --check` before and after the rebase; new endpoint verified live returning the expected 401 without identity headers; new frontend function names confirmed present in the deployed `index.html`; confirmed the purged dev-cache file no longer serves its real content (just the Worker's generic fallback text). **Left open:** haven't visually verified the heatmap in a real browser with real multi-person data (no way to simulate multiple people's submissions from this session) — worth Lujane doing a quick look once a few club members have added their availability.

**2026-09-25 (Stop Cloudflare edge-caching the app shell — fixes "my link changes still aren't live") — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane reported, more than once, that a routing/link change I'd already deployed and verified via curl still didn't show up for her on her phone — most recently "still no custom links, im on arts n crafts club and it's the same normal link" even though a fresh `curl` proved the code was live and correct. **Found:** `curl -sI https://4dasistas.ca/` showed `cf-cache-status: HIT` on the main page — Cloudflare's edge was caching `index.html` (the app shell), so depending which edge node a visitor's request landed on, they could keep getting an OLD cached copy of the page (and therefore the old JS) for a while after a deploy, even though the origin already had the fix. My own verification curls used a `?nc=<random>` cache-buster each time, which bypasses Cloudflare's cache key — so *I* was always seeing the fresh version while a real visitor on a bare URL could still be served stale. **Fix:** the Worker now force-sets `Cache-Control: no-store, must-revalidate` on its response for the shell paths only — `/`, `/index.html`, `/service-worker.js`, `/manifest.webmanifest` — right after `env.ASSETS.fetch(request)`, so the edge is told never to cache these regardless of the origin asset's own header. Everything else (images, `/data/*.json`, fonts) is untouched and keeps its normal caching. `no-store` is one of the few directives Cloudflare honors even under a zone-level "Cache Everything" rule, so this should hold regardless of how the zone's caching is configured (I don't have dashboard access to check that directly this session). **Not fully certain:** whether this alone fully explains every "still broken" report this session — it's the most concrete, verifiable cause I found (the routing code itself checked out correct in every click-through test), but if she still sees stale behavior after this ships, the next place to look is a Cloudflare dashboard Cache Rule / Page Rule I can't see from here, and a full cache purge would help either way.

**2026-09-25 ("Add your availability" 10-day grid on each club's idea-board page) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: under the existing sign-in-with-name / suggest-and-vote idea board on each club's page, add "Add your availability" — always the next 10 days in an Apple-Calendar-style week grid, tap-or-drag to mark Morning/Afternoon/Evening, plus a per-day button that cycles Available all day (green) → Busy all day (red) → clear. **Scope decision:** this is open to the SAME audience as voting — a signed-in member or a name-only guest (`ideaWho()`), not gated behind full club membership/PIN signup like the existing month-view calendar on the "You're a member of the X Club" screen (`clubavailability:*`, untouched). **Backend:** new `GET/PUT /api/clubs/:clubId/idea-availability`, same identity check as the ideas routes (public, no admin gate), one private KV doc per person per club (`ideaavail:<clubId>:<personId>`, `personId` = `m:<userId>` or `g:<guestId>` — reuses `ideaWho()`/`ideaClub()` already in scope). Value shape: `{"YYYY-MM-DD": "busy" | ["morning","afternoon","evening"]}` — `"busy"` = the Busy-all-day button (renders solid red); an array of all 3 parts = the Available-all-day button OR manually dragging all 3 (renders solid green, no need to distinguish how it got there); 1–2 parts = partial, each shown in its own colour (reusing the site's existing morning/afternoon/night palette — yellow/orange/blue — renamed **Evening** here per this request, the existing month-view calendar still says "Night", untouched). New `sanitizeIdeaAvailDays()` mirrors the month-view's `sanitizeAvailabilityDays()` pattern (max 60 dates, ±3/+45 day window, drops anything malformed instead of erroring the whole request). **Frontend:** `ideaAvailSectionHtml()` renders under the ideas cloud in `renderClubIdeas()` (prompts "Enter your name above" if not signed in yet); the grid is a CSS grid (label gutter + 10 day columns × 4 rows) inside its own horizontally-scrolling box so it never overflows the page even on a 320px phone. Drag-to-paint uses `pointerdown` (real target) + a `window`-level `pointermove` using `elementFromPoint(clientX,clientY)` (not `pointerenter`, which doesn't bubble, and not per-cell listeners, since the grid's inner HTML is replaced on every toggle) — the same idiom already used elsewhere in this file for other drag features. `touch-action:none` on the segment cells so a phone drag paints instead of scrolling the page (the grid's own horizontal scroll still works from outside the cells). Saves debounce 700ms (`ideaAvailScheduleSave`/`ideaAvailSave`, mirrors `availScheduleSave`/`availSave`). Reset alongside the rest of the idea-board state in `openClubIdeas()` and on rename/rejoin. Tested against a local Worker: the 10-cell drag paints every intermediate cell (verified with real multi-step pointer events, not just a coarse two-point drag), the all-day button cycles correctly, values persist across a reload, a second guest sees an empty board (per-person privacy), no overlap/overflow at 320–375px including the "Available all day" full-green rendering. **Not done:** no aggregate/"who's free when" view for admins — only your own availability is shown to you; say if she wants that next.

**2026-09-21 (iPhone: admin add/edit forms are now a full-screen sheet with a sticky Save bar) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: adding new events still feels bad on iPhone. Can't run a real iPhone here; fixed the known iOS causes and verified in Chrome emulation at 375px. **Causes found:** (1) the form scrolled inside a scroller (`#detail-view` overflow) inside another (`.detail-card` `max-height:calc(100vh - 60px)`); iOS Safari's `100vh` includes the area behind its toolbar, so the bottom of the card (Create/Save) sat behind the toolbar and the two scrollers fought each other and the keyboard; (2) Create/Save only at the very bottom of a long form; (3) 13–14px inputs, 12px labels and ~13px checkboxes (tiny tap targets; sub-16px inputs also trigger iOS focus-zoom in some browsers); (4) the floating ✕ sat on top of fields while scrolling; (5) my earlier `appearance:none` on date/time inputs can render empty date fields as blank/collapsed on iOS. **Fix:** `#detail-view` gets an `is-form` class in `renderEventEditView` / `renderResourceEditView` / `renderAboutEditView` (removed in `renderDetail`/`closeDetail`); at ≤520px that makes the form a full-screen sheet (`100dvh`, safe-area padding, ONE scroller, `overscroll-behavior:contain`), hides the floating ✕ (Cancel buttons exist), 16px inputs/selects with ≥44px height, 22px checkboxes, bigger labels/back button; new `.ev-actions` sticky bottom bar (Create/Save + Cancel) for the event and listing forms, always visible with safe-area padding; date/time inputs keep native rendering (`width:100%;min-width:0;min-height:44px`). Normal event detail pages unchanged (only `100vh`→`100dvh` on their max-height). Checked in emulation: one scroller, bar visible at every scroll position, Save tappable (46px), no overflow/overlap in create + edit + listing forms, Delete still reachable. **Not verified:** on an actual iPhone (keyboard behaviour, the Google address dropdown inside the sheet).

**2026-09-21 (fix: idea-board / sign-up Submit doing nothing; Cuisine board = cuisine type + place + map link) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Two things. **(A) Bug:** a friend kept pressing submit on an entry and nothing happened. **Most likely cause (reproduced by reading/testing, not observed on her device):** the idea-board form's map box was `<input type="url">`; browsers validate that natively *before* our submit handler runs, so any text that isn't a strict URL (e.g. `www.google.com/maps/…` without https://, or the place name pasted in front of the link, which is what Google Maps' share sheet often gives) silently blocked the submit — phone/in-app browsers (Instagram's especially) show no validation bubble, so it just looked dead. Even when our code did run, errors were plain small text. Also the server only accepted a few Google hosts and only `https://`. **Fix:** forms are `novalidate`, the box is `type="text" inputmode="url"`; new `cleanMapLink()` pulls the link out of whatever was pasted (name + link, no https, http) and the Worker's `isGoogleMapsUrl` does the same and now also accepts `share.google`, `g.page`, `maps.google.<tld>`; errors show in a red box and scroll into view (`ideasSetStatus(msg,true)`); the sign-up form's error/“username taken” messages also scroll into view. Lookalike hosts (`maps.app.goo.gl.evil.com`), Apple Maps, search URLs are still rejected (with a clearer message). **(B) Cuisine board:** `club-cuisine` add form now has 3 required fields — Cuisine type, Name of the place (= idea title), Google Maps link ("Add restaurant"); the Worker requires `cuisine` (2–30 chars) for that club only (`CUISINE_CLUB_ID`) and stores/returns it (`cuisine` on ideas); bubbles show "🍽 CUISINE" above the name, every bubble with a map link gets a 📍 mini-button that opens Google Maps directly, the ⓘ popup shows the cuisine chip, a big "Open in Google Maps" button and the link text; admin edit has a Cuisine field. Other clubs' forms unchanged apart from (A). Tested against a local Worker (wrangler dev --local) + the real page at 375px: empty/missing-field/garbage messages, pasted-with-name links, the 14 link shapes via API, sign-up regression. **Not verified:** the friend's real device, and whether the production KV daily-write quota was involved (couldn't check without writing to the live board).

Routing stays hash-based (`#/…`, works on the static host, no Worker change). New `currentSlugParts()` (state → link) and `applySlugParts()` (link → state) replace the ad-hoc code in `syncStateToHash`/`restoreStateFromHash`. **Links:** `#/calendar`, `/calendar/<sports|activities|functions|trips|knowledge>`, `/calendar/sports/<sport>`, `/calendar/activities/<city>`, `/calendar/trips/<domestic|international>`, plus `/list`, `/week`, `/day` for the view; `#/clubs`, `/clubs/join` (the sign-up form, was `#/fallsignup`), `/clubs/start`, `/clubs/guidelines`, `/clubs/<club>` (idea board), `/clubs/<club>/members`; `#/resources`, `/resources/<category>`, `/resources/<category>/list`; `#/travel`, `/travel/<domestic|international>`, `/travel/past`; `#/about`, `#/subscribe`, `#/add-to-homescreen`; `#/event/<id>` unchanged. **Old links still work** (#/fallsignup, #/clubstart, #/clubjoin, #/homescreen, #/calendar/mosqueprograms, the `#tab=…` query format) and get rewritten to the new form. **New behaviour:** the `hashchange` handler now switches page when a different page's link is pasted/clicked or Back/Forward is used (before, it only handled event/club-board links and otherwise closed the overlay and wiped the hash); pages needing setup (`clubmembers`, `clubideas`) go through `enterDeepLinkState()`, also used after the first data load. **2026-09-20 (Monthly repeat option for events) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`, `scripts/generate_calendar.py`** — Lujane: events can repeat monthly, not just weekly. **Data model (all optional, on the source `data/calendar/*.json`):** `recurFrequency:"monthly"` + either `monthlyType:"weekday"` with `monthlyWeek` (`"1"`–`"4"` or `"last"`) and `monthlyDay` (`sun`…`sat`), e.g. 2nd Saturday; or `monthlyType:"date"` with `monthlyDate` (1–31; months without that day are skipped, same as iCal). Weekly is unchanged (`days`); monthly items keep `days` empty. `recurStart`/`recurEnd` bound either kind; the event's own date (`eventDate`/`calDate`) is always shown and is the default start. **Places updated:** (1) site calendar — `occurrencesInMonth` + new `monthlyDatesInMonth`/`monthlyRuleLabel`, and the displayed date becomes e.g. "Every 2nd Saturday of the month"; (2) `worker.js` `eventIsOnDate` (via `monthlyRuleMatches`) so "today's events" includes monthly ones; (3) `generate_calendar.py` emits `RRULE:FREQ=MONTHLY;BYDAY=2SA` / `BYDAY=-1FR` / `BYMONTHDAY=15` (+ `UNTIL` from `recurEnd`); the in-page custom ICS export does the same; (4) admin form: new **Repeats** dropdown (Doesn't repeat / Weekly / Monthly) with the weekday checkboxes under Weekly, the monthly rule builder under Monthly (with a live preview line), and "Repeat starts/ends" (renamed from Recurring). Existing weekly events open as Weekly. Validation: monthly-by-date needs 1–31. `eventHasDate()` counts monthly as having a date. **2026-09-20 (Travel globe: trips in the same country no longer overlap) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: two trips in the same country must never overlap on the globe; place them in different spots in the same country. **Cause:** pins are drawn at each trip's `lat/lng`, and several trips share (or nearly share) coordinates — both Jordan trips, both Granada trips, both Macedonia/Kosovo trips, and three Morocco trips (0.6–1° apart). **Fix:** new `spreadTripPoints()` (with `TRIP_PIN_MIN_SEP = 2.6°`) runs in `renderTripEarthView` on a copy of the points and iteratively pushes any pins closer than that apart (longitude scaled by cos(lat)); exact duplicates split in a fixed repeatable direction. The trip data is never modified, only where the pin is drawn, so it applies to every future trip too. Pin radius 1.5→1.0 and pulse ring max 6→3 so the pins/rings themselves don't touch. Checked: all 19 mapped trips now ≥2.6° apart, biggest shift 1.6°; 6 identical points also separate. Not verified: that shifted pins stay inside each country's borders (a ~1.3° nudge can cross a border in small countries like Jordan). Note: `barbados-treat` has no lat/lng so it isn't on the globe at all.

The separate "Details change often…" `#pageDisclaimer` sits *below* the Subscribe button and was left alone — say if that one should go too. **Gap:** `main` had 80px bottom padding; new `body.on-events main{padding-bottom:14px}` with `on-events` toggled in `render()` — calendar-to-Subscribe gap 84px → 18px. **Travel:** added `← Back to Home` (`#travelBackBtn`, `data-hometab="home"`) at the top of the Travel page; shows however you reach it. About has no back button (not asked). Tested locally at 375px.

**2026-09-20 (emoji-id events open; event form: no description, required date+location, Canada-only search) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane reported (1) some events can't be opened (e.g. "Take a Hike..Into Fall"), (2) new-event form: drop description, make date + location required, (3) phone overlap in the time/all-day row and event buttons, (4) location must be the search box only, virtual stays a checkbox, Canada-only unless it's a trip. **(1) Root cause:** events whose `id` starts with an emoji (`🍂take-a-hike-into-fall`, `🌲don-valley-hike`, `🥾she-travels-hike`…) never opened on click: browsers percent-encode non-ASCII in `location.hash`, and the `hashchange` handler / initial-load code passed the still-encoded id (`%F0%9F%8D%82…`) to `renderDetail`, so `findItem` missed. Fixed with `eventIdFromHash()` (decodeURIComponent, try/catch) used by both. **(2)** Create mode no longer renders the Description textarea (edit mode of existing events keeps it); Event date and Location show a red `*`, and `saveEventEdit` validates title → date → location (unless Virtual) *before* the request, keeping everything typed. Worker `POST /api/admin/calendar-event` now also rejects a non-virtual event with no location (date was already required server-side). **(4)** Location is a hidden `#editLocation` fed only by the Google Places search (`mountEventLocationSearch`, "📍 chosen address" line under it); `includedRegionCodes:['ca']` unless the Calendar tab is Trips (re-mounted when the tab select changes); ticking Virtual hides the block and saves `location:''`. If Google Maps fails to load the input becomes a plain text box so admins are never blocked. **(3)** Date/time row labels now `flex:1 1 130px;min-width:0` with `flex-wrap`, and date/time inputs get `min-width:0;max-width:100%;appearance:none` (iOS Safari gives native pickers a wide intrinsic size). Tested locally at 375px and 320px with a mocked admin session: no overlaps/overflow, validation messages, Canada vs trips element config, virtual save payload. Not tested on a real iPhone.

**2026-09-19 (admin: Add another event after creating; mobile overlap fixes) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: as admin, after adding an event she had to X out and reopen to add the next one, and the mobile view must not overlap anything. **Flow:** after a successful create the form now shows a green "✓ Event created!" box with **➕ Add another event** (calls `openEventCreate()`: fresh blank form, scrolled to top, `eventJustCreated` reset) and **Done** (`closeEventEdit(false)`); while editing an existing event (not review mode, not create mode) there is also a **➕ Add a new event** button under Save/Cancel. New state flag `eventJustCreated`. **Mobile:** (1) the admin footer buttons (New event / N need details / Admin: Log Out) were absolutely positioned top-right and overlapped the social icons at ≤520px — they are now a static centered wrapping row there; (2) the Time / End time / All day row in the event form didn't wrap, so "All day" overflowed the card by ~6px at 375px (sideways scroll) — now `flex-wrap:wrap`. Audited the create-event, edit-event and add-business forms at 375px with a script that checks every pair of visible controls for overlap and the card for horizontal overflow: none found after the fixes. Tested with a mocked admin session and mocked save.

**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-19 (idea boards v2: name-only sign-in, map links, voter names, admin edit; per-club links; Clubs removed from calendar)

**2026-09-19 (idea boards v2: name-only sign-in, map links, voter names, admin edit; per-club links; Clubs removed from calendar) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane's asks: admin can edit votes + a date on top; people sign in without a password (no real account) to vote/add; option names viewable; adding an option needs a title AND a Google Maps link; remove Clubs from the calendar and the "See … events on the calendar" line; every club gets its own bubble-vote page and designated link. **Identity:** a signed-in member acts under their account name; anyone else joins with just a name → a random guest id kept in localStorage (`ideaGuest`) sent as `X-Guest-Id` + URL-encoded `X-Guest-Name` (CORS allows them). This is trust-based: the id is not verified, so someone can vote twice by clearing storage/using another device — mitigated by 3 options per person, 40 per club, an in-memory per-IP throttle (40 writes/min, costs no KV writes), and the admin controls; true one-person-one-vote would need accounts. Votes are now `{id,name}` objects (names shown in the ⓘ popup with the place link and who added it); old string-vote data still reads (shown as "Member"). **Options:** `title` (3–80) + `mapUrl` required, validated server-side to an https Google Maps host allowlist (maps.app.goo.gl, maps.google.com, google.*/maps, goo.gl/maps, g.co/kgs) — `javascript:`/other hosts rejected. **Admin** (session cookie, routes below the `requiresAuth` gate): `PUT /api/admin/club-ideas/:clubId/:ideaId` edits title / mapUrl / `date` (YYYY-MM-DD, shown at the TOP of the bubble) / `votes` (sets the displayed total via an `adjust` offset; real named voters stay; later real votes add on top); DELETE removes; ✎ edit + ✕ buttons appear on bubbles when `cmAdminLoggedIn`. **Per-club pages/links:** `CLUB_SLUGS` → `#/clubs/active-gaming|adrenaline|theatre|cuisine|arts-crafts|field|adhd`; deep links load the board directly (`restoreStateFromHash`), pasting one while the app is open works (`hashchange`), `syncStateToHash` writes it, boards show the link with a Copy link button; the Clubs grid now shows all 7 clubs (`clubsForGrid()` adds ADHD, which isn't in data/clubs.json, from `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS`). **Calendar:** removed Clubs from it entirely — `clubCalendarItems()` deleted (no club had calendar events), no longer in today's list/search index, the clubs sub-filter and old club-card→calendar path removed, and the "See … events on the calendar" link deleted. Note: `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS`, `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS`, and `CLUB_SLUGS` must be updated together when a club is added. **Tested** with local `wrangler dev` + throwaway `.dev.vars` (deleted): curl (guest w/o name 401, missing/evil/javascript map links 400, unicode guest name, admin PUT 401 without cookie then edit/validation, adjust math), and UI at 375px (deep link for every club, all 7 cards, guest join/short name, add-option validation, vote grows bubble, ⓘ popup names + map link, admin edit saved date/votes/title). Not tried on production with real people.

**Status:** IDLE

**Last updated:** 2026-09-19 (Clubs: sign-out button, tighter subtitle gap, Mentimeter-style club idea boards)

**2026-09-19 (Clubs: sign-out button, tighter subtitle gap, Mentimeter-style club idea boards) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Three asks from Lujane. **(1) Sign out:** signed-in members get a "Sign out" pill in the top-right of the Clubs-tab panel (`cmMemberSignOut` — member session only; the footer "Sign Out" still clears member + admin). **(2) Gap under the subtitle:** it was 36px on every tab (16px `.intro-line` margin + 20px `main` padding-top); now `.intro-line{margin:16px auto 8px}` and `main{padding-top:6px}` (12px on ≥768px) → 14px on every tab (measured). **(3) Idea boards:** pressing a club card on the Clubs tab now opens a new `clubideas` view ("Ideas for the X Club") instead of jumping to the calendar — the old behaviour is one tap away via "See the X events on the calendar →". Signed-in members type a new event idea (3–80 chars) and everyone signed in taps a bubble to vote (tap again to un-vote); bubbles are outlined pastel circles (8 colour pairs from a hash of the idea id) whose diameter grows with votes (112px → max 210px, longer titles get a bigger minimum so text isn't cut off) with the title and "N votes" inside; the tap animates the growth optimistically then re-sorts (biggest first). Logged-out visitors can view but get "Log in to vote". Own ideas (and any idea for a logged-in admin) show a ✕ to remove. **Worker (all under `/api/clubs/:clubId/ideas`):** GET (public; Bearer optional → `voted`/`mine`), POST suggest (member; creator auto-votes; max 3 per member, 40 per club, case-insensitive duplicate → 409), POST `/:ideaId/vote` (toggle), DELETE own; plus `DELETE /api/admin/club-ideas/:clubId/:ideaId` (admin cookie) placed BELOW the `requiresAuth` gate (I first put it above by mistake, caught and fixed before committing; verified: unauthenticated call → 401). clubId must be in `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS` (all 7 clubs). Storage: one KV doc per club `clubideas:<clubId>` = `{ideas:[{id,title,by,at,votes:[userId]}]}`; simultaneous votes are read-modify-write so a rare race could drop a vote — fine at this scale. Anyone signed in (any club) can suggest/vote on any club's board. **Tested** with local `wrangler dev` + throwaway `.dev.vars` (deleted): API auth/validation/dup/caps/permissions via curl; UI at 375px logged-out (view-only, login prompt) and logged-in (suggest, vote grows bubble 112→146px, duplicate message, remove own idea, Sign out returns Sign Up/Log In tiles). Not tried on production with real members.

**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-19 (Sign Up form creates the login + puts them in their #1 club; other picks = standby)

**2026-09-19 (Sign Up form creates the login + puts them in their #1 club; other picks = standby) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: people should pick their own name + PIN on the sign-up form and be immediately in their first-choice club with the calendar showing; their other two picks should read as standby. **Worker:** new public `POST /api/signup` ({name, username, pin (4 digits), clubId, waitlist:[…]}) — validates, creates the user, adds them to that ONE club, returns the same shape as `/api/login` (token, clubs) plus `waitlist`. Reverses the old "public self-signup is intentionally disabled" decision on purpose (Lujane asked), so it is guarded: `clubId`/waitlist ids must be in `SIGNUP_CLUB_IDS` (mirrors `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS` — update both together if a club is added), 10 sign-ups/hour per IP and 100/day overall (KV counters `signuprate:*`/`signupday:*`; the daily cap also protects the KV free-tier write budget), username uniqueness (409), name control-chars stripped. Waitlist is stored on the user (`user.waitlist`); `/api/login` and `/api/signup` return `visibleWaitlist()` = waitlist minus any club they're now a member of, so when an admin adds them to a club (existing admin panel) the standby note disappears on their next login. **Frontend:** the Sign Up form (`fallsignup`) has a new "Create your login" box (username + PIN; the PIN input has no `name`, so it is never sent to Formspree). Submit order: validate → `/api/signup` (a taken username is fixed before anything is sent) → Formspree post (organizers still get the full form incl. ranked picks, WhatsApp, username; if Formspree fails the member still gets in and sees a notice to DM us) → `setCmSession` → Clubs tab with the panel open. Panel now shows a green welcome banner (username reminder), "You're a member of the <#1 club>", a yellow standby note for picks #2/#3, then the month view. `avClubTitle` falls back to `FALL_SIGNUP_CLUBS` (ADHD club has no entry in data/clubs.json). Also fixed a mobile regression from my all-caps nav change: at ≤480px the four tabs + search overflowed and were clipped; tabs now fit one row (11px) and search is a full-width row below. **Tested** with local `wrangler dev` + throwaway `.dev.vars` (deleted): real form → signed in to #1 club with standby for #2/#3, PIN absent from the Formspree payload, taken username keeps typed values, validation/unknown club/bad pin/name errors, waitlist de-dup + filtering, login returns waitlist, admin add drops it from standby, rate limit returns 429. Not yet tried on production with a real signup. Known leftover: the form hero still says "submit by Monday, August 24th" (stale, not changed). Login has no attempt limiting (4-digit PINs + public roster usernames) — pre-existing, worth a follow-up.

**2026-09-16 (Delete an event entirely, from the Needs-details review and from any Edit Event) — Cline (VS Code) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane: "when i press the need details button in admin login i want to be able to delete the event entirely, add that option". The review flow could only fix or skip an item, so a junk/duplicate event stayed on the site forever. Added a real delete path. Worker: new `githubDeleteFile(env, path, sha, message)` helper alongside `githubPutFile`, and the existing `/api/admin/calendar-event/:id` route now accepts **DELETE** as well as GET/PUT (same handler, same `CALENDAR_ID_RE` id validation, same `resolveCalendarFile` slug-fallback lookup, so the id/filename mismatch cases work too). It re-uses the file's own sha from the GET, commits a `Delete "<title>" via site admin editor` message as the same "4DASISTAS Site Admin" committer, and returns `{ok, deleted, path}`. Auth needs no new wiring: `requiresAuth` already covers `path.startsWith("/api/admin/calendar-event")`, and the CORS `Access-Control-Allow-Methods` already listed DELETE. Frontend: a red **"🗑 Delete this event entirely"** button now sits under the Save/Cancel row of the event editor — in the Needs-details review *and* in any normal Edit Event, but deliberately **not** in Create mode. Clicking it asks `confirm()` first ("removes it from the site for good — the calendar, its month, and the downloadable feed… cannot be undone"), then DELETEs, then `purgeItemInMemory(id)` drops it from every in-memory collection (all the section arrays plus ALL_ITEMS) so the list, `renderDetail`, the review-queue count and the "Needs details" badge all stop showing it immediately instead of waiting for a reload. In review mode it also decrements `reviewTotal` and calls `advanceReviewQueue()`, so deleting walks you straight to the next one; outside review mode it closes the editor and re-renders. `purgeItemInMemory` is deliberately NOT called until the server confirms, so a failed/declined delete leaves the page exactly as it was. Deleting the source file also triggers the repo's existing `regenerate-calendar.yml` workflow (push on `data/calendar/**`), which rebuilds the aggregate data + `calendar.ics` without it. Validated: a jsdom harness driving the real DOM (**35/35 assertions**: button presence/label/hint in both modes, absent in create mode, DELETE URL, purge from ALL_ITEMS *and* from the owning data array, reviewTotal decrement, auto-advance, exit-on-empty, detail overlay closing, deleted title gone from the list HTML, and that declining `confirm()` deletes nothing) plus a Worker unit harness importing the real module with a mocked `fetch`/KV (**20/20**: 401 when unauthenticated with zero GitHub calls, happy-path 200 with the right repo path + sha + branch + commit message + committer, 404 on missing source file with no GitHub DELETE attempted, 502 surfaced on GitHub failure, 400 on an invalid id with no GitHub round-trip, and GET/PUT regression checks). Both `node --check` clean. NOT yet deployed.

**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-15 (fix "Event source file not found" for pre-migration event ids)

**2026-09-15 (fix "Event source file not found" for pre-migration event ids) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane hit "Error: Event source file not found" clicking Save & Next mid-review on a real event. Root cause: the admin editor's GET/PUT routes assumed `data/calendar/<id>.json` always matches the filename exactly, but a couple of events predate the folder-collection migration and kept their original (non-slug) `id` inside the file while the file itself got saved under a slugified name — e.g. id `"MuslimahFarmerMarket"` actually lives in `muslimahfarmermarket.json`, and id `"Padel_SisterthroughFaith"` lives in `padel-sisterthroughfaith.json`. GitHub's file paths are case-sensitive, so the direct lookup 404'd. First checked whether these were genuinely missing/at-risk-of-deletion (initially miscounted them as orphaned using a flawed filename-based check, which led me to almost create duplicate source files — caught it before committing by running `scripts/build_content.py` locally, which correctly errored on the exact duplicate-id collision it's designed to catch) — confirmed both already have a real, correct source file; the bug was purely in how the Worker resolved id → path. Fixed with a `resolveCalendarFile(env, id)` helper: try `data/calendar/<id>.json` first (matches every new event, including ones created via the new "New event" button), and fall back to `data/calendar/<slugify(id)>.json` if that 404s — reusing the exact same `slugify()` already used for event creation, since that's precisely the transformation these old filenames went through. Scanned all 150+ real event ids: exactly these 2 have a filename≠id mismatch, and both are now correctly resolved by the fallback (verified live against the real GitHub-hosted files, not just a mock). The PUT path now correctly writes back to whichever path was actually resolved, not a freshly-recomputed (and wrong) path, so an edit can never create a duplicate file at the wrong location.

**2026-09-15 (admin editor: create new events + full field parity with Decap) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — DecapBridge is throwing "Failed to persist entry: API_ERROR: Requires authentication" on publish (still unresolved — GitHub OAuth/App re-auth needed on Lujane's end, see the previous entry), so as a workaround she asked for full event *creation* through the site's own admin login too, with nothing missing versus the real CMS form. Two changes: (1) New Worker route `POST /api/admin/calendar-event` creates a brand-new `data/calendar/:id.json` file via the GitHub Contents API (PUT with no `sha`, which is how you create rather than update) — derives a unique id/filename from the title the same way Decap's `slug: '{{title}}'` does (lowercase, strip punctuation, collapse to hyphens), auto-appending `-2`, `-3`, etc. on a collision (checked by probing GitHub before writing), validates Title/Calendar-tab/Event-date are present, and never writes an `id` key into the file itself (the filename IS the id, matching how every other source file already works). (2) The in-site form itself, which previously covered ~17 of the ~21 real CMS fields and explicitly said so, now has full parity: added Extra one-off dates (dynamic add/remove list of date pickers, stored as `[{date:...}]` matching Decap's list-widget shape), Sport key + "also list under these other sports" (dynamic list, sports-only — a small section that shows/hides itself when the Calendar-tab dropdown is switched to/from Sports), and "Also show in these other calendars" (checkboxes for the same 5 sections Decap itself offers there, correctly excluding Support Groups since Decap's own option list excludes it too). Root cause avoided proactively: the dynamic add/remove buttons for the two list fields call a new shared `collectEventFormFields()` (also now used by the normal Save path) to snapshot every current field's value into state BEFORE mutating the list and re-rendering the whole form — without this, clicking "+ Add another date" would have silently wiped out anything already typed into Title/Description/etc., since the form is rebuilt from state on every render. A single "New event" footer button next to Admin Login opens a blank form (sensible defaults, no fetch needed since there's nothing to load yet); saving switches it seamlessly into normal edit mode for the same event afterward. Validated: worker-side validation branches (missing title/section/date, invalid section, unauthenticated, bad-token-graceful-502) all tested via local `wrangler dev`; frontend tested end-to-end in-browser with a mocked fetch — form renders all new fields, section-change toggles the sports-only block, typed values survive an add/remove round-trip, a full create submission succeeds and correctly flips the form into edit mode with the new id, and Cancel-before-save closes cleanly with no id set yet.

**2026-09-14 (missing-details review queue in the admin editor) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: give me a section in admin that finds events missing details (e.g. audience type) and prompts me to fill them in one by one. Built directly on top of the calendar-event editor from earlier today. `missingDetailsFor(e)` flags an event if it's missing Audience Type (empty array — matches the CMS's own `required: true` on this field), Description, or Location (skipped for virtual events). A new footer button "📋 N need details" appears next to Admin Login whenever logged in and the count is nonzero; clicking it starts a review queue — opens the same edit form used for a single event, but in review mode it shows a "Reviewing X of Y" banner, red-outlines the specific missing field(s) with a "— missing" label, and swaps the buttons to "Save & next" (saves then auto-advances after a beat) and "Skip this one" (advances without saving) instead of Save/Cancel; "Exit review" leaves the queue at any point. Also keeps the in-memory copy of each item in sync after a save (`Object.assign` onto the `findItem()` result) so the remaining-count badge ticks down live within the same session without needing a reload, and re-checks the queue fresh each time it's opened so already-fixed events don't reappear. Scope note (told to Lujane implicitly via the existing "some advanced fields aren't editable here" line already in the form): this only scans the 6 editable calendar sections, not Resources/Small Businesses. Validated end-to-end in-browser with a mocked fetch (real GITHUB_TOKEN writes not needed to verify the flow logic): confirmed the queue builds correctly (138 of ~200 test items flagged), Skip advances without saving, Save & Next saves + syncs the in-memory item + auto-advances, the footer badge count ticks down live after each save, and Exit cleanly closes and refreshes the badge.


**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-14 (persist current tab across a home-screen app relaunch)

**2026-09-14 (persist current tab across a home-screen app relaunch) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane: "when I'm on a specific tab and I refresh it takes me to the original, I want it to stay where I was." The site already round-trips tab/filter state through the URL hash on a normal reload (verified this still works fine on its own) — the actual gap is that relaunching a home-screen PWA always opens the bare `start_url` ("/") with no hash at all, by how iOS launches installed web apps, which a hash-only mechanism can never survive. Mirrored `syncStateToHash()`'s params to `localStorage` (`lastAppState`) on every change and cleared on default Home state, and made `restoreStateFromHash()` fall back to the saved copy when the URL has no hash at all. Also confirms in passing: the calendar-event editor from 2026-09-14 (GITHUB_TOKEN root-cause fix) is now genuinely working in production — 3 real edits (ISNA Thrive, Balancing Deen & Study, ISNA Youth Book Club) landed as real commits from "4DASISTAS Site Admin" and synced through to the aggregates correctly, including one that changed an event's calendar tab (functions → Knowledge) and correctly moved it between the generated files. Validated: JS syntax check; in-browser test navigating to a tab then reloading with a hash-stripped URL (simulating a fresh app relaunch) correctly restored the tab from localStorage, and returning to Home correctly cleared the saved state so it doesn't get "stuck".


**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-14 (found root cause: every `wrangler secret put` this session hit the wrong Worker script)

**2026-09-14 (found root cause: every `wrangler secret put` this session hit the wrong Worker script) — Claude (chat) — `workers/wrangler.toml`** — Lujane reported two things back to back: the footer admin password "wrong" even though she typed the exact one given earlier, and the new calendar-event editor still saying "GITHUB_TOKEN is not set" despite it being set live. Root cause, found via `workers_list`/`workers_get_worker`/`wrangler secret list --name <x>`: there are TWO separate Cloudflare Worker scripts on this account — `4dasistas` (the one actually bound to the `4dasistas.ca/api/*` routes and serving real traffic) and `4dasistas-editor` (an unused leftover, apparently the original script name from before some earlier rename that `workers/wrangler.toml` was never updated to match — its `name` field still said `4dasistas-editor`). Every `wrangler secret put`/`wrangler deploy` run from this repo's `workers/` folder this session — the 2026-08-29 `ADMIN_PASSWORD` reset AND today's `GITHUB_TOKEN` — silently landed on the dead `4dasistas-editor` script instead of live `4dasistas`, which is why the "122139" password never actually worked and why GITHUB_TOKEN kept showing as unset in production even after I set it (and even after I'd independently verified the raw PAT worked directly against GitHub's API — that check bypassed the Worker entirely and never would have caught a wrong-script mismatch). **This does NOT mean the deployed CODE was ever wrong** — the actual worker.js source has been correctly deployed to live `4dasistas` this whole session via Cloudflare's own git-triggered build on every push (confirmed repeatedly via `workers_get_worker_code`); only secrets set via my local `wrangler` CLI were misdirected. Fixed: (1) re-set `GITHUB_TOKEN` on `4dasistas` directly via `--name 4dasistas` override — confirmed via `wrangler secret list` it's now present there; (2) deleted the stray `ADMIN_PASSWORD`/`GITHUB_TOKEN` secrets left on `4dasistas-editor` to prevent future confusion; (3) fixed `workers/wrangler.toml`'s `name` field from `4dasistas-editor` to `4dasistas` so this can't recur. **Still outstanding**: the live `4dasistas` script's actual current `ADMIN_PASSWORD` is unknown (it has SOME value set from before this whole mix-up, not "122139") — asked Lujane for a fresh password to set correctly this time now that the script-name bug is fixed. Also separately investigating (not yet resolved): Lujane reported search-suggestion results "covered" on her phone — reproduced the search dropdown live on a 375px mobile viewport and it rendered correctly (fixed position, z-index 9998, right below the search bar, nothing covering it), so this may be specific to real iOS Safari/standalone-mode behavior I can't fully reproduce headlessly; needs more detail from her (which page/tab, does it happen in the home-screen app or regular Safari too) to pin down.


**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-14 (lock pinch/double-tap zoom + service-worker cache gap on /admin, /api)

**2026-09-14 (lock pinch/double-tap zoom + service-worker cache gap on /admin, /api) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `service-worker.js`** — Two requests. (1) Lujane wants the home-screen app to feel native, not zoomable: added `maximum-scale=1.0, minimum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no` to the viewport meta (blocks pinch-zoom) plus `touch-action:manipulation` on html/body (blocks double-tap-zoom as a fallback, since some iOS versions ignore `user-scalable=no` for accessibility in full Safari — less of a concern in true standalone/home-screen mode, which is closer to a native WebView). (2) Lujane reported "error verification" repeatedly when trying to Publish a new Knowledge program via DecapBridge. Most likely cause, given the earlier 2026-09-12 log entry: DecapBridge's GitHub OAuth connection is probably still tied to the old `lugine` username from before the 2026-09-12 rename to `lujaneyaffa`, and this is a continuation of that unresolved issue rather than something new — told Lujane to try logging out of DecapBridge and back in, or revoking + reauthorizing its GitHub OAuth App from https://github.com/settings/applications. While investigating I found and fixed a real, separate, compounding bug: `service-worker.js`'s fetch handler cached (and could serve stale) EVERY same-origin GET including `/admin/*`, completely ignoring the `Cache-Control: no-store` rules already set in `_headers` for exactly this reason back on 2026-08-25 — those `_headers` rules only stop the *browser's* HTTP cache, the service worker's own `cache.put()` doesn't check Cache-Control at all. Added an explicit early-return in the fetch handler for `/admin`, `/api`, and `/editor` so those paths are always network-only (never cached, never served from cache), and bumped `CACHE_NAME` to v6 so the fix takes effect promptly (skipWaiting+clients.claim were already in place, so no double-refresh needed). This may or may not be the actual cause of "error verification" specifically (that string reads more like an OAuth/identity-token verification failure at DecapBridge's gateway than a stale-page symptom) — flagging both possibilities to Lujane rather than claiming certainty, and asking her to report back the exact behavior after both reauthorizing AND getting this deploy. Validated: `node --check` on both files; viewport meta tag confirmed present via grep.


**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-14 (footer admin login fixed for iOS standalone mode + safe-area insets)

**2026-09-14 (footer admin login fixed for iOS standalone mode + safe-area insets) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane set GITHUB_TOKEN live (`wrangler secret put`, verified the PAT can read the repo directly via GitHub's API), then reported two problems testing the new footer admin login as her iPhone's home-screen app: "incorrect password, won't let me try again" and "view is messed up." Root cause of the login issue: the login UI was a small popover anchored under the footer button (`position:absolute` near the bottom of a long scrolling page) with a plain `<button>` (not `type=submit`) — in an iOS standalone PWA, the on-screen keyboard covering the bottom of the viewport plus the popover's anchor position made it easy for the Sign In button to be pushed off-screen/unreachable after the first attempt, and there was no real `<form>` so the iOS keyboard's "Go" action had no native submit to hook into. Replaced it with a fixed, viewport-centered modal (a real `<form>` with `type="submit"`, `enterkeyhint="go"`, and `autocomplete="current-password" autocapitalize="none" autocorrect="off"` to stop iOS from mangling the typed password) that stays visible and reachable regardless of scroll position or keyboard state; confirmed via direct event-dispatch testing that a failed login now correctly keeps the modal open with a visible error, ready to retry immediately (an earlier coordinate-based click/type test appeared to show it silently closing, but that was purely a browser-automation targeting quirk, not a real bug in the shipped code — dispatching the actual submit event confirmed correct behavior). Separately, and likely the actual cause of "view is messed up": the site has `viewport-fit=cover` + `apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style: black-translucent` (has for a while, not something I added) but NO safe-area-inset handling anywhere, meaning in standalone/home-screen mode the page renders edge-to-edge under the iPhone's status bar/notch with nothing pushing content clear of it — added `env(safe-area-inset-top)` to the header's top padding and `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` to the footer's bottom padding and the new admin modal's padding, a real pre-existing gap, not a regression from this session's footer changes (verified no visual regression on a normal 375px mobile viewport before this fix). Validated: JS syntax check, in-browser modal open/cancel/backdrop-click/failed-submit-retry all confirmed working via direct DOM/event testing.


**Status:** IDLE
**Last updated by:** Claude (chat)
**Last updated:** 2026-09-13 (in-site calendar event editor — DecapBridge-alike)

**2026-09-13 (in-site calendar event editor — DecapBridge-alike) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Built the "full replacement" option Lujane picked over DecapBridge: a small "🔒 Admin Login" button now sits in the top-right corner of the footer (replaces having to know a hidden URL); logging in there uses the same ADMIN_PASSWORD/session-cookie as `/editor`. Once logged in, every calendar event's detail popup (Sports/Activities/Functions/Trips/Knowledge/Support Groups — not Resources yet) gains an "✏️ Edit event" button that opens an in-page form covering the ~20 most-used fields (title, section/calendar-tab, audience type, emoji, organizer, dates, times, all-day, recurring weekdays/range, virtual, location, link, description). Root architectural fact this rests on: the live site NEVER reads Cloudflare KV for content — it only reads the static `data/*.json` files built from git, so an edit only "counts" if it becomes a real GitHub commit, exactly like Decap/DecapBridge already do. New Worker endpoints `GET/PUT /api/admin/calendar-event/:id` (session-cookie gated, same as other admin routes) use the GitHub Contents API directly: PUT does a read-modify-write (merges just the form's fields into the existing file so untouched fields like extraDates/sportKey/alsoShowIn are never lost), commits straight to `data/calendar/:id.json` on main. That commit triggers the existing `regenerate-calendar.yml` Action to rebuild the aggregates + calendar.ics (its own follow-up commit), which Cloudflare then deploys — so a save takes roughly a minute or two to actually appear live, not instantly; the UI says so after saving. Security: added a new `GITHUB_TOKEN` secret requirement (fine-grained PAT, Contents read/write, scoped to just this repo — NOT the broad personal `gh` OAuth token) so the Worker's write access is least-privilege; the id in the URL is validated by blocking `/`, `\`, and control characters (not an allowlist — first pass used a character allowlist that both rejected 6 real event ids with curly apostrophes/emoji-variation-selectors AND was the wrong shape of defense; switched to blocking path-traversal characters directly, verified all 151 real ids now pass while `../../workers/src/worker` style payloads are still rejected). Validated: local `wrangler dev` confirms the auth guard (401 logged-out), fail-closed GITHUB_TOKEN check (500 misconfigured), and graceful 404 on a bad/placeholder token rather than a crash; in-browser testing confirmed the footer button/popover, the Edit button's visibility gating, and the form's field population/collection all work correctly. **Not yet functional in production** — needs Lujane to create a fine-grained GitHub PAT (Settings → Developer settings → Fine-grained tokens → this repo only → Contents: Read and write) and give me the value to `wrangler secret put GITHUB_TOKEN`; until then the feature fails closed with a clear "Server misconfigured" error rather than silently doing nothing. Not yet covered: Resources/Small Businesses editing, and a few advanced calendar fields (extraDates list, sportKey/alsoSportKeys, alsoShowIn) which still require DecapBridge — flagged in the edit form itself so Lujane knows what still needs the old tool.

**2026-09-12 (stored XSS fix — member photo field) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Security audit requested by Lujane ("is this website hackable?") found a real stored-XSS vulnerability: `sanitizePhoto()` only checked the `data:image/` prefix and length, so a club member could set their own profile photo (self-service via `PUT /api/users/:id` with their own Bearer token) to a value containing a `"` that broke out of the `<img src="...">` attribute wherever photos are rendered — the public club roster/dashboard (`index.html:3072`, `3079`, `3570`) and the `/editor` admin panel's Club Members overview (`worker.js` loadClubMembers script), the only two places that pattern was used; every other member field there was already escaped, just not this one. Impact: injected script in the public roster context could read `cmSession` from localStorage (member tokens aren't HttpOnly) for full member-account takeover by anyone viewing the roster; in the admin `/editor` context it would run inside the admin's authenticated session and could issue arbitrary fetches with `credentials:'include'` (e.g. rewrite site content via `/api/data/:key`, delete members) even though the session cookie itself is HttpOnly. Fixed at the source — `sanitizePhoto()` now requires the full value to match `^data:image\/(png|jpe?g|gif|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/]+=*$` (blocks any quote/angle-bracket injection) — plus defense-in-depth: escaped the photo value at all 4 render sites (3 in `index.html`, 1 in the worker's admin panel) that previously interpolated it raw. Checked production KV: only one real member record exists (Lujane's own), photo field was already `null` — no pre-existing malicious data to clean up. Validated: unit-tested the new regex against both the exploit string and a real base64 PNG (blocks the former, passes the latter); confirmed via local `wrangler dev` that the PUT route and auth guard still execute correctly on the changed code path.

**2026-08-30 (calendar controls fix) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Fixed the 3 top controls on the Events calendar (List/Calendar toggle, Day/Week/Month, Event Types) not aligning well. Root cause: 3 different implementations with different padding/border - List/Calendar used a bordered container with borderless buttons inside, Day/Week/Month was a native select dropdown with its own border, Event Types was a standalone button with yet another border width/color. Converted Day/Week/Month from a select dropdown into real buttons reusing the exact .view-toggle class (same container+button pattern as List/Calendar), and standardized Event Types' border/padding to match both. Added matching mobile-size overrides so alignment holds on phone too. Tested via runtime simulation - confirmed clicking Week correctly updates state and active class.

**2026-08-30 (Club Members Schedule: admin club editing, grid day labels, best-time, availability suggestions, signed-in button) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Five requests. (1) Admin's per-member Edit form (in the All Members overview) now includes a checkbox list of every club — checking/unchecking and saving adds or removes that membership. New backend route `PUT /api/admin/users/:id/clubs` takes the full desired club-id list, diffs it against `allClubIdsContaining()`, adds/removes only what changed, and — consistent with the existing single-club delete behavior — fully releases the identity/username if the edit leaves them in zero clubs. (2) The event availability grid's header row now shows the weekday abbreviation above each date (e.g. "Sat 09-05"), not just the bare date. (3) The "Who's free" aggregate view now shows a top-right "Best time: <day date, time> — N free" line, computed from the response aggregate (ties show "+N more"). (4) **Availability suggestion for multi-club members** — clarified with Lujane that this should mean "reuse the weekday+time pattern from other events I've already answered," not a separate one-time template. Implemented as a client-side pattern cache (`localStorage`, keyed per member) built from every slot they've ever saved, keyed by weekday+half-hour rather than exact date; when a member who's in more than one club opens an event they haven't answered yet, if the pattern has any matches for that event's actual date range, a dismissible banner offers to apply them — never auto-fills silently. (5) The general "Log In" button now reads "You're signed in as {name}" once a session exists, and clicking it in that state skips the login form entirely — one club opens straight to its dashboard, multiple shows the existing "pick your club" list, zero explains they're not in any club yet. Verified all five end-to-end via curl + in-browser: admin add/remove-club via checkboxes confirmed against the live roster endpoints, grid header shows correct weekday for known dates, best-time text computed correctly from a seeded aggregate, the suggestion banner correctly proposed and applied a Saturday-evening slot saved on one club's event onto a different club's Saturday event, and the button label/behavior verified in both the signed-out and signed-in states.

**2026-08-29 (production KV data migration after global-identity rearchitecture) — Claude (chat) — production KV data only, no code change** — Lujane reported "no members even though I added someone" right after the global-identity rearchitecture shipped. Root cause: the member she'd added (Lujane/@lujane in Active Gaming) was written under the *old* per-club schema (a `clubmembers:<clubId>` value holding full member objects with inline `pinHash`), but the new code expects that value to be a plain array of `user:<id>` reference strings — so `resolveClubMembers` silently resolved to nothing for every existing club. Confirmed by reading the live KV directly (`npx wrangler kv key list/get ... --remote` — note the default without `--remote` reads the *local* preview namespace, which briefly looked like the account had zero data and cost some time to catch). **Also found two junk entries in `clubmembers:club-adhd`**: one was literally my own earlier deploy-verification curl probe (`probe` / `@deploycheckxyz`) that got created directly against production instead of local dev — a mistake worth flagging so no future agent repeats it: never let a "confirm the route exists" check use a request shape that could actually succeed and persist data, hit it with an input designed to be rejected (or better, read-only). After getting explicit confirmation from Lujane before touching production data directly, migrated the real record (new `user:<id>` with the identical name/username/pinHash so her existing PIN keeps working, fixed `username:lujane` to point at the id directly, rewrote `clubmembers:club-activegaming` to `["<id>"]`) and cleared the two junk `club-adhd` entries (emptied the membership list, deleted the stray `username:deploycheckxyz` index). Verified via the live public roster endpoint: Active Gaming now shows Lujane, ADHD now shows zero members (correct — nobody real has joined it yet).

**2026-08-29 (Club Members Schedule: global identity, multi-club login) — Claude (chat) — `workers/src/worker.js`, `index.html`** — Lujane pointed out the general "Log In" button shouldn't ask which club a member is in, because the same person could belong to several clubs — and under the previous data model that was actually impossible to do cleanly: each club stored its own fully separate member record, so even though usernames were already globally unique, the same real person would need a *different* username per club, defeating the point. **Rearchitected member identity to be global**: a new `user:<userId>` KV record (name, username, pinHash, photo) is the single source of truth for a person; `clubmembers:<clubId>` now just holds a list of userIds (who belongs to that club), so one identity can appear in as many clubs as needed. Sign-in is now `POST /api/login` (no clubId in the URL) — given a username+PIN it returns the person's profile plus `clubs: [clubId, ...]`, every club they're actually in. Member sessions (`membersession:<token>`) are now scoped to a userId, not a (clubId, memberId) pair. Frontend: the general "Log In" button shows username+PIN directly (no club dropdown) — after signing in, one club opens straight to its dashboard, multiple clubs shows a small picker limited to *their* clubs only (not the full club list), zero clubs shows a friendly "ask an organizer" message. The per-club "Club Members Schedule" card button still works standalone: if the signed-in person isn't actually in that specific club, they see a clear "you're not a member of this club yet" screen instead of silently working or erroring. Admin side: creating a member with a username that already exists now just adds that existing person to the new club (keeping their current PIN) instead of rejecting as "taken" — the create form has a hint explaining this; deleting a member now only unassigns them from that one club, and their global identity + username are only fully released once they're removed from every club they were in. Verified end-to-end via curl (multi-club global login, partial delete preserving the identity in remaining clubs, full delete releasing the username for reuse) and in-browser (signed in as a member added to two clubs, picker showed only her two clubs, opening one skipped straight to its dashboard with no re-login, and attempting a third club she wasn't in correctly showed the not-a-member screen).

Removed the now-redundant per-club "Members" list that used to live under the club dropdown — the club selector is still there, but scoped now to just Create Member + Events management for that club, since the overview covers viewing/editing every member already. **2026-08-29 (Club Members Schedule rework: usernames, per-event availability, in-site admin) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`** — Lujane requested four changes to the Club Members Schedule feature shipped earlier today, amounting to a near-total rework of its data model: (1) profile-picture upload moved out of the initial create-profile screen — members now add/change it later from inside the dashboard, once signed in. (2) Added a real `username` field distinct from display `name`; usernames are globally unique across every club (enforced via a new `username:<name>` KV index, checked/reserved at signup, released on admin delete) — login is now by username+PIN instead of name+PIN. (3) **The whole "My availability" concept changed from a generic recurring weekly grid to admin-created, per-event scheduling** — a small "🔒 Admin" button now sits under the Gals Clubs grid; clicking it prompts for the existing site admin password (new JSON endpoints `/api/admin/login`, `/api/admin/logout`, `/api/admin/session` reuse the exact same session-cookie mechanism `/editor` already used, so there's only one admin password, not a second system) and opens an inline panel to pick a club and create events (title, description, date range, hour range) or delete existing ones. Members now see their club's admin-created events on their dashboard and open one to mark availability specifically for it — with a "Who's free" aggregate/heatmap mode (click a slot to see who's marked it) alongside their own editable "My availability" grid. (4) Grid granularity changed from four generic blocks (morning/afternoon/evening/night) to real hourly labels with 30-minute cells (0-47 half-hour indices per day), selectable by click-and-drag across a range (pointerdown+pointerenter painting, snapping to whichever on/off state the first cell in the drag was toggled to) — verified this drag logic directly via dispatched PointerEvents in-browser (the browser-automation tool's synthetic mouse drag doesn't fire real PointerEvents, so it couldn't be visually drag-tested pixel-by-pixel, but the underlying handler logic was confirmed correct: multi-cell paint-on, multi-cell paint-off, and click-to-toggle all verified). **Bug caught and fixed during testing**: after saving your own availability, switching to "Who's free" showed stale data (still fetched from when the event was first opened) until the event was reopened — fixed by refetching responses right after a successful save. Backend: replaced the old per-member `availability` object entirely with per-club-event response records (`clubevents:<clubId>` for event definitions, `clubeventresponses:<clubId>:<eventId>` for member responses, aggregated server-side into counts). Verified end-to-end via curl against a real local KV (global username uniqueness incl. cross-club collision + release-on-delete, username+PIN login, photo update after login, admin login/session-check/unauthenticated-401, event create/list/delete, slot save with garbage-input sanitization dropping invalid dates/out-of-range hours) plus the full visual flow in-browser (admin creates event → member signs up with name+username → PIN reveal mentions username → dashboard shows in-place photo upload + roster with @handles + events list → opens event → hourly/half-hour grid renders correctly → click-to-toggle and drag-paint both function → save → aggregate view updates immediately).

**2026-08-29 (Club Members Schedule, new feature) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `workers/src/worker.js`, `.gitignore`** — New feature per Lujane's request: a private, per-club "Club Members Schedule" page (when2meet-style). Every Gals Club card now has a second button, "Club Members Schedule", next to Sign Up. Clicking it (no site-wide gate — this replaces an earlier master-PIN design Lujane walked back) opens a create-profile-or-sign-in screen for that specific club: a new member enters their name (+ optional profile picture, downscaled client-side to a 200×200 JPEG before upload) and the SERVER generates them a personal 4-digit PIN, shown once on screen ("write this down"); returning members sign back in with name+PIN. Once in, they see their club's member roster (click any name to view that person's schedule read-only) and their own editable weekly availability grid (Morning/Afternoon/Evening/Night × Sun–Sat, click-to-toggle, Save button). **Architecture**: this needed real multi-user writable storage, which the site didn't have before (it's static JSON + Decap CMS) — reused the existing `SITE_DATA` KV namespace and the Worker's existing session-cookie infrastructure (same pattern as `/editor`'s admin login) rather than standing up new infra. Data model: one KV key per club (`clubmembers:<clubId>`) holding that club's full member array (id, name, SHA-256-hashed PIN, optional photo data-URI, availability object); a separate `membersession:<token>` KV entry per signed-in device (180-day TTL) is what the client actually sends on writes (`Authorization: Bearer <token>`), not the raw PIN, so the PIN itself is only needed for name+PIN login on a new device. New public routes: `GET/POST /api/clubs/:clubId/members`, `POST /api/clubs/:clubId/login`, `GET/PUT /api/clubs/:clubId/members/:id`. **Admin capability** (Lujane's explicit ask — "needs admin login which would just be me and I can change their pins"): reused the existing `/editor` admin-password session gate to add `GET /api/admin/club-members` (every club's roster) and `POST .../reset-pin` (issues + returns a fresh PIN for a member who's lost theirs — old PIN is invalidated immediately) plus a `DELETE` to remove a member; a small management panel was added to the bottom of the existing `/editor` page. Duplicate names within one club are rejected at signup (login is by name+PIN, so names must be unique per club) with a message pointing them to sign in instead. Verified the entire flow (create profile → PIN reveal → sign-in with wrong PIN rejected → sign-in with right PIN → write availability without a token rejected 401 → write with token succeeds → availability sanitized against garbage day/block keys → second member created and appears in roster → clicking their name shows their schedule read-only → session survives a page reload via localStorage → admin list/reset-pin/delete all work and old PIN stops working immediately after reset) using `wrangler dev --local` against a real (local, disposable) KV store, plus a full visual pass in-browser for the member-facing screens. Note: admin-panel *browser* login couldn't be visually screenshotted locally because the session cookie is `Secure`-flagged and browsers refuse to set `Secure` cookies over plain `http://localhost` — this is a pre-existing property of the site's `/editor` auth, not something new; confirmed the admin routes work correctly via direct `curl` instead, and it'll behave normally on the real `https://4dasistas.ca`. Caveat for Lujane: this is a soft-security PIN system appropriate for a small club roster (SHA-256 hashed, no salt) — fine for keeping casual public visitors out, not meant to withstand a determined attacker; also photos are capped at ~225KB each and stored inline in the per-club KV entry, so a club with many members all adding large photos could eventually approach KV's per-value size ceiling (25MB) — not a near-term concern, but worth knowing if it ever comes up.

**2026-08-29 (emoji titles + organizer removal) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`, `admin/config.yml`** — Two requests from Lujane. (1) Bug: organizer names were still showing in the Home Day/Week compact boxes despite an earlier "city-only" pass — traced it to two render templates that pass had missed (`renderCalDayWeek`'s `eventRow` and the homepage's `todayEventRow`; the month-grid day-click popup had already been fixed previously) — removed the `item.organizedBy` span from both. The flat list-view card and the detail modal intentionally kept `organizedBy`, since Lujane's complaint was specifically about the week-view boxes. (2) Feature: every event now gets an automatic leading emoji before its title, in priority order: manual `emoji` override → sport (via `sportKey`, e.g. badminton→🏸, boxing→🥊) → keyword match against title+desc (hike→🌲, picnic/potluck→🧺, BBQ→🍖, Quran/halaqa→📖, etc., ~25 rules) → country flag for Trips (matches country/city names in title+location, e.g. Jordan→🇯🇴) → section fallback (sports→🏅, trips→✈️, mosqueprograms→🕌, etc.). Applied via a single `emojiTitle(item)` helper at all 10 places `item.title` is rendered (list cards, compact boxes, month-grid pips + popup, search suggestions, travel globe/list, detail modal). Added an optional "Emoji override" text field (pattern-limited to 1-8 UTF-16 units, enough headroom for ZWJ/flag sequences) to all 6 calendar collections in `admin/config.yml`, right after Title, so editors can override a wrong auto-pick. `scripts/build_content.py` needed no change — it already passes all fields through untouched. **Caught two bugs during testing, not obvious from code review:** the manual override was silently non-functional whenever a title already had a baked-in emoji character (the "don't double up" guard checked title-has-emoji before ever checking `item.emoji`) — fixed by checking the override first; and the hike keyword regex missed "Muslim Hikers" (only matched "hike"/"hiking", not "hikers") — broadened to `hik(e|ing|ers?)`. Verified in-browser via `emojiTitle()` against real production data across all 6 sections (sport items, keyword items, a Jordan trip, items that already had emoji baked into their titles to confirm no double-prefixing, override-vs-no-override on the same item), plus a full visual pass of the Home Week view confirming emojis render correctly and no organizer names remain. Note for editors: if a title already has an emoji typed into it AND an override is also set, both will show — remove the emoji from the Title text itself if setting an override.

Found two separate causes: (1) the month-grid's "click a day" popup (`renderCalendarDayDetail`) still used the old `eventTypeLabel · locationLabel` line I'd missed when doing the city-only change earlier — fixed to use `cityLabel()` like every other box now does. (2) A genuine data gap: 11 real GTA venues had no city in their `location` text at all (just the venue name, e.g. `"Masjid Al-Ezz"`, `"Angela James Arena"`, `"ISM Main"`, `"Padel Junction"`, `"Lions Valley Park"`, `"Amana Event Centre and Banquet Hall"`, `"Erin Hill Acres"`), so `cityLabel()`'s known-city match correctly found nothing. **Verified each venue's real city via web search (not guessed)** and fixed the location text with sourced addresses: Angela James Arena/Tennis Court → Toronto (165 Grenoble Dr), Masjid Al-Ezz → Mississauga (10 Falconer Dr), ISM Main → Markham (2900 Denison St), Padel Junction → Mississauga (1780 Sismet Rd), Lions Valley Park → Oakville (1227 Lions Valley Rd), Amana Event Centre → Scarborough (2855 Markham Rd), Erin Hill Acres → Hillsburgh, ON (a real farm well outside the GTA — left as a non-GTA city on purpose, didn't force a fake local one). Separately, improved `cityLabel()`'s fallback for genuine non-GTA locations (international trips, rural venues) to show the last meaningful comma-segment (usually country/region, e.g. "Spain", "Jordan", "Tokyo") instead of blank — caught and fixed a real bug in this fallback mid-testing where a combined "ON N0B 1Z0" province+postal segment wasn't being skipped (regex only matched an exact 2-letter code, not one followed by a postal code) before landing on the wrong answer for Erin Hill Acres. Ran a full scan afterward: only **one** item now has no derivable city anywhere in the dataset — "Pink Track Sunday" has no `location` field at all, its description explicitly defers exact location to an external app ("Details on SweatPals"). Did not guess a city for it despite `organizedBy: "womensfitmississauga"` hinting at Mississauga — that's an organizer name, not a confirmed venue, and Lujane's ask was explicitly for accuracy. Flagging this one for her to fill in if she knows the real venue.

**2026-08-27 (city labels, modal, calendar-tab view) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Three requests from Lujane. (1) Day/Week event boxes on Home now show ONLY the city under the title (was "Type · full address") — added `cityLabel(item)`, which prefers a structured `item.city` field where it exists (currently only some Activities entries) and otherwise matches against a `KNOWN_GTA_CITIES` list against `item.location`'s freeform text (addresses are wildly inconsistent — no street/city/province comma position is reliable across the data, so exact parsing isn't feasible; this heuristic is good-enough for a GTA-only dataset). (3) Added the identical Day/Week/Month view selector to the Calendar tab (next to the existing List/Calendar view toggle, visible only when "Calendar view" is active) — generalized `homepageTodayItems`/`homepageWeekDays` into candidate-parameterized `itemsOnDate`/`buildWeekDays` so the new `renderCalDayWeek()` can reuse them with `itemsForCategory(state.categoryFilter)` instead of the homepage's fixed "everything" set, meaning Day/Week here correctly respects whatever category filter (Sports/Activities/etc.) is active. Reused all the same CSS classes/see-more/scrollbar/mouse-drag behavior verbatim via `cal`-prefixed element ids to avoid any collision with the homepage's version, which remains untouched and still works standalone. Verified in-browser: city-only labels render, modal opens/closes via button and backdrop-click while nav stays visible, calendar-tab Day/Week/Month all render and switch correctly, category filtering flows through to the new views, and clicking an event inside the calendar-tab's week strip opens the same shared modal.

**2026-08-27 (week strip fix) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Root-caused Lujane's "only Saturdays show" report on the homepage Week view: Cline's `multi`/`multi-wide` classes (previous commit `29e6b0f`) gave busy days (>4 items → 2-col, >8 → 100% width) a much wider column than normal days. In the live data, Saturday and Sunday both had >8 items, so each took the *entire* strip width on mobile — meaning after loading, the user's viewport showed only that one column, and reaching any other day required multiple full-screen swipes, making it look like nothing else existed. Replaced that whole variable-width approach per Lujane's request: every `.week-day-col` is now a uniform, smaller fixed width (`calc((100% - 30px)/4)`, ~4 visible per screen instead of 3), each day caps at 4 visible event rows with a `+N more` button that reveals the rest inline (`.week-hidden-items`, toggles to "Show less"), and added a custom thin scrollbar (`.week-scrollbar`/`-thumb`, synced to `.week-panel`'s scroll position/width ratio on scroll) so the horizontal-scroll affordance is visible, not just the existing text hint. Verified in-browser at 375px: all columns render at uniform ~79px width, busy days correctly show 4 events + "+N more", toggle works both directions, scrollbar thumb width/position tracks actual scroll state.

**2026-08-27 (missed deploy) — Claude (chat) — no code changes, investigation only** — Lujane reported Cline's "fan busy days into multi-column lists" commit (`29e6b0f`) wasn't showing live despite Cline saying it pushed cleanly. Confirmed the commit is really on `main`, its diff is small and completely benign (pure CSS + one JS variable, no touched scripts/config), and it works correctly when tested locally (`multi`/`multi-wide` classes apply correctly by event count). **Proved this was a genuinely missed deploy, not "still building"**: checked the `4dasistas` Worker's actual `modified_on` timestamp via the Cloudflare API directly — its last deploy was `20:52:15 UTC`, which lines up almost exactly (56s) with the *previous* commit `96779b0` (`20:51:19 UTC`), but `29e6b0f` (`21:00:13 UTC`) had 9+ minutes pass with zero deploy activity. So the pipeline successfully built the commit right before it, then silently missed this one — most likely a one-off dropped GitHub webhook delivery or a flaky Cloudflare build-runner pickup, not anything wrong with the code or the build command config. This is a new failure mode we haven't seen before (previous "not deploying" incidents were config problems, not intermittent misses) — worth watching for a repeat. **2026-08-27 (merge reconciliation) — Cline (VS Code) — `index.html`** — Resolved the rebase conflict between Claude's 105px-mobile week-column fix and my container-relative week strip. Final combined behavior: Week view is a continuous 31-day strip (today → +30) that slides freely via native horizontal scroll (`overflow-x:auto` + `overscroll-behavior-x:contain`, scrollbar hidden). Columns are `flex:0 0 calc((100% - 20px)/3)` max-width 150px → exactly 3 full columns at every phone width (verified 320–414px incl. padding); this is more robust than Claude's 105px fixed override, which still clips on a 320px iPhone SE, so that 105px media rule was removed as dead code. Swipe: in Week mode the `touchmove` handler no longer `preventDefault()`s and `touchend` no-ops → the strip free-slides with natural momentum (this was the "rigid" swipe complaint); Day mode keeps Claude's horizontal-lock + always-move-by-1-day-per-swipe for precise control. ‹/› arrows `scrollBy` one viewport (smooth) in week mode, prev enabled; heading "WHAT'S ON"; date range spans the whole strip. Validated: `node --check` + column-fit arithmetic. NOTE: not deployed live — Cloudflare deploy is still manual.

**2026-08-27 (swipe simplified) — Claude (chat) — `index.html`** — Lujane reported the animated swipe I just built caused runaway repeated jumps ('keeps going and going'). Found the actual bug: the previous version used a 180ms setTimeout before updating state, creating a window where a second quick swipe (very likely, given the first gave no clear feedback) could schedule an overlapping second state update, stacking jumps. Reverted to a simple, immediate (no-delay) swipe with no CSS drag animation - removes that whole bug class. Also fixed the step size: swipe now always moves by exactly 1 day regardless of Day/Week view mode (was jumping 7 days at once in week view, which is what actually prompted her original report - she wanted to see 'just the next two days', not skip a whole week). Arrow buttons still use the day/week-aware step size, unchanged, since that wasn't part of the complaint.
